THE GUARD FAILS BOTH WAYS (AN UNEVIDENCED CANONICAL FIELD; A SCANNED FIELD WITH NO DISPOSITION) AND RE-READS EACH NAMED LEGACY FILE, SO THE FROZEN LEGACY TREE IS THE DRIFT DETECTOR — WHICH IMMEDIATELY CAUGHT FOUR FIELDS RECORDED AGAINST THE WRONG FILE. **A SCHEMA IS TIGHTENED ONLY ON EVIDENCE**: `eid` FROM §9.1, DATES AS ISO `DateOnly` (THE DRIVER OWNS `MM/DD/YYYY`), `ssn`/`postalCode`/`state`/PHONES FROM VERIFIED LEGACY VALIDATORS; EVERY OTHER FIELD CARRIES CONSERVATIVE TEXT PLUS `evidence:"audit-at-migration"` AND A REQUIRED `auditNote`, BECAUSE A SCHEMA WRONG IN THE **TIGHT** DIRECTION REJECTS REAL OPERATOR DATA AND `/^\d{4,6}$/` FOR `deptId` WOULD BE THE UNVERIFIED CONSTANT THIS TREE BANS. `ppsEid` IS A SEPARATELY BRANDED FIELD WITH **NO** DECLARED CONVERSION TO `Eid`. REDACTION IS DECLARED PER FIELD AND REFUSED AT DEFINITION TIME BY DOC 11's OWN TABLE, SO THE DURABLE MANIFEST HOLDS A REDACTED CELL WHILE THE LIVE GRID SHOWS THE OPERATOR THE REAL ONE FROM THE CONTENT-ADDRESSED SOURCE — `ssn`/`dob` USE `digest`, SINCE THE LAST FOUR OF AN SSN IS ITSELF AN IDENTIFIER. TWO STRUCTURAL LOCKS: `defineCanonicalField` REFUSES ANY `example` ITS OWN COERCE+PARSE REJECTS, AND NO NORMALIZED HEADER ALIAS MAY CLAIM TWO FIELDS — AN AMBIGUOUS ALIAS MAKES THE §2.5 SCORER'S TOP HIT ARBITRARY, WHICH IS §7 VECTOR #1 IN DISGUISE. `IntakeManifestId` IS THE ONE MANIFEST IDENTITY; §5's `IntakePlanId` IS THE SAME THING UNDER AN EARLIER NAME. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §1.1 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §9; `src/domain/fields/`; `config/canonical-field-inventory.json`; `tests/unit/architecture/intake-canonical-field-registry.test.ts`; `tests/unit/canonical-fields.test.ts` |
| **D147** | **THE SUGGESTION SCORER CANNOT REACH A BINDING, AND MAPPING REUSE RESOLVES BY HEADER IDENTITY OR NOT AT ALL.** DOC ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.5's "SUGGESTIONS ONLY" IS NOW A TYPE FACT: A `ColumnBinding` REQUIRES `confirmedAt`, A `Suggestion` HAS NO SUCH FIELD AND NO WAY TO ACQUIRE ONE, AND `confirmBinding` IS THE SINGLE MINT — SO NO CODE PATH RUNS FROM THE SCORER TO A POPULATED `bindings` ARRAY (§7 #1). THE SCORER GAINS ONE SIGNAL LEGACY'S REGEXES NEVER HAD — THE COLUMN'S REAL SAMPLES TRIED THROUGH THE CANONICAL FIELD'S OWN COERCE+SCHEMA — AND A HEADER THAT MATCHES WHILE **NO** SAMPLE PARSES SCORES **ZERO**, BECAUSE THAT COMBINATION IS A MIS-MAP RATHER THAN A WEAK MATCH; A `CellCoercionError` THERE IS THE MEASUREMENT AND ANYTHING ELSE PROPAGATES. CANDIDATES WITHIN 0.05 ARE `ambiguous` AND NEVER PRESELECT. `defineIntakeProjection` PROVES §1's INVARIANT BY **OBJECT IDENTITY** — A BYTE-IDENTICAL SCHEMA COPY IS REFUSED, SINCE TWO IDENTICAL SCHEMAS DRIFT APART INVISIBLY AND LEGACY ALREADY SHIPPED THAT EXACT DEFECT (§7 #6) — UNWRAPS OPTIONAL/DEFAULT/CATCH (BUT NOT `nullable`) TO DECIDE REQUIRED-NESS, AND FOLDS EACH TARGET'S SCHEMA HASH INTO `intakeProjectionFingerprint` SO A MAPPING BUILT AGAINST A LOOSER CANONICAL SCHEMA CANNOT SURVIVE ITS TIGHTENING. REUSE REFUSES OUTRIGHT ON WORKFLOW/PROJECTION/LAYOUT MISMATCH (§2.3's "A NEAR-MISS LAYOUT CAN NEVER PARTIALLY REUSE"), THEN RESOLVES EACH BINDING BY NORMALIZED HEADER PLUS OCCURRENCE INTO `held` / `confirmation-required` / `unbound`; **THE STORED INDEX NEVER BINDS** (§7 #3) AND EXISTS ONLY TO REPORT THAT A COLUMN MOVED. `SourceColumnId` USES CANONICAL JSON RATHER THAN DELIMITER CONCATENATION BECAUSE A HEADER MAY CONTAIN THE DELIMITER. THE WHOLE `src/domain/intake` TREE STAYS FREE OF NODE BUILT-INS — THE MAPPING GRID IS A BROWSER SURFACE (DOC ref frontend-backend-integration §8). | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.6 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.1–§2.5 + §7; `src/domain/intake/`; `src/validation/schema-path.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/intake-mapping-boundary.test.ts`; `tests/unit/intake-mapping.test.ts` |
| **D148** | **THE INTAKE MANIFEST STORES CURRENT DISPOSITION AND DERIVES EVERY TOTAL FROM IT, AND A REJECT ID IS DERIVED SO REVALIDATION IS IDEMPOTENT.** `rejectedRows` IS IMMUTABLE HISTORY — A CORRECTED ROW KEEPS ITS REJECTION EVENT AND LOSES ONLY ITS **CURRENT** REJECT IDS — SO TOTALS CANNOT BE ACCUMULATED BESIDE THE TRUTH; THEY ARE COMPUTED FROM EXACTLY ONE DISPOSITION PER SOURCE ROW AND THE SCHEMA REFUSES ANY MANIFEST WHERE THE TWO DISAGREE, WHICH IS §7 #12 MADE MECHANICAL. A REJECT ID FOLDS `(SOURCE DIGEST, ROW, KIND, SUBJECT)`, SO A SECOND VALIDATION PASS OVER A CORRECTED FILE PRODUCES THE SAME IDS INSTEAD OF A DUPLICATE EVENT PER PASS, AND `buildIntakeManifest` THROWS RATHER THAN EMITTING A MANIFEST IN WHICH A ROW IS NEITHER VALID, REJECTED NOR EXCLUDED. VALIDATION IS **FORMAT-AGNOSTIC** (A PARSED CELL MATRIX IN, DISPOSITIONS OUT), SO ITS INVARIANTS ARE PROVABLE WITHOUT A FILE. `attemptCoercion` RETURNS A DISCRIMINATED VALUE — THE `catch` CLASSIFIES AND RETURNS, SETS NO FLAG AND SUPPLIES NO DEFAULT — SO A REFUSED CELL BECOMES A DURABLE LOCATED REJECTION WHILE A FAULT IN A COERCER STILL REACHES THE TOP. A BLANK **OPTIONAL** CELL IS GENUINE ABSENCE; A ROOT-LEVEL SCHEMA ISSUE IS `cross-field` NAMING EVERY BOUND TARGET (TRUTHFUL, WHERE INVENTING THE SPECIFIC PAIR ZOD NEVER REPORTED WOULD BE A GUESS); `duplicate-identity` REJECTS **BOTH** COLLIDING ROWS, SINCE PICKING A WINNER SILENTLY DROPS A PERSON THE OPERATOR LISTED. TWO REFINEMENTS ON §5's TEXT: THE MANIFEST PINS `canonicalInputSchemaHash` ONCE SO A READER PROVES WHICH SCHEMA PRODUCED `parsedInput` RATHER THAN INFERRING IT (D62/D110), AND `enqueueReadiness` SEPARATES `unresolved-rejections` FROM `no-valid-rows` SO A VALID ZERO-ROW INTAKE STAYS BLOCKED AND INSPECTABLE INSTEAD OF REPORTING A NO-OP SUCCESS. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5.0 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5 + §7 #11/#12; `src/domain/intake/manifest.ts`; `src/domain/intake/validate.ts`; `tests/unit/intake-validation.test.ts` |
| **D149** | **THE EXTRACTION STORE'S ID FOLLOWS DOC 01's VERB GRAMMAR, ITS PARSERS WERE CHOSEN AS A SAFETY DECISION, AND A CELL IS CANONICALIZED TO TEXT AT EXACTLY ONE EDGE.** DOC ref data-intake-and-edit-data §3's ILLUSTRATIVE `extraction/parse-csv` DOES NOT SATISFY DOC 01's READ VERBS (`read|search|list|extract|match|verify`) AND DOC ref task-contract §3.4 ALREADY WRITES `extraction/extract-pdf-fields`, SO THE ID IS CORRECTED TO **`extraction/extract-spreadsheet`** RATHER THAN THE GRAMMAR BEING WIDENED; ONE TASK COVERS `.csv` AND `.xlsx` SO NO CALLER BRANCHES ON A FILE EXTENSION. `read-excel-file@9.3.5` + `csv-parse@7.0.2` (BOTH MIT, ACTIVELY MAINTAINED, FOUR AND **ZERO** RUNTIME DEPENDENCIES; csv-parse IS STRICT BY DEFAULT SO A RAGGED ROW THROWS RATHER THAN BEING PADDED). **`xlsx` (SHEETJS CE) IS REFUSED**: ABANDONED ON npm AT 0.18.5 WITH TWO UNFIXED HIGH-SEVERITY ADVISORIES INCLUDING PROTOTYPE POLLUTION ON A CRAFTED FILE (CVE-2023-30533), AND THIS PROCESS PARSES OPERATOR-SUPPLIED FILES WHILE DRIVING REAL UCPATH/KUALI WRITES. ALSO REFUSED: `node-xlsx` (RESOLVES SHEETJS THROUGH A `cdn.sheetjs.com` TARBALL URL, INVISIBLE TO `npm audit`), `exceljs` (WHAT LEGACY USES; UNMAINTAINED SINCE 2023, NINE RUNTIME DEPS), `@office-kit/xlsx` (BEST-DESIGNED BUT PRE-1.0 ALPHA WITH A DECLARED-UNSTABLE API). A DATE-**FORMATTED** CELL BECOMES ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` FROM ITS UTC COMPONENTS AT THIS EDGE AND NOWHERE ELSE, SO `parseUsOrIsoDate` CAN REFUSE A BARE EXCEL SERIAL AS A LOUD PER-CELL REJECTION INSTEAD OF A 1900-EPOCH GUESS; AN UNDOCUMENTED CELL TYPE THROWS RATHER THAN BECOMING `[object Object]`. HEADER DISCOVERY IS **ALIAS-INDEPENDENT** (STRUCTURE DECIDES; ALIASES ADD AT MOST 0.15) AND PRESELECTS ONLY ON BOTH A FLOOR AND A MARGIN, SO INDISTINGUISHABLE ROWS RETURN A CHOICE — PICKING WRONG SHIFTS EVERY COLUMN BY ONE AND SURFACES AS FORTY UNRELATED BAD CELLS. WRITING ITS TESTS FOUND A REAL RANKING DEFECT: `MM/DD/YYYY` DID NOT COUNT AS DATA-SHAPED, PUTTING EVERY ORDINARY EXPORT INTO "AMBIGUOUS". **`scenario-coverage` NOW COLLECTS REFERENCES FROM THE TASK STORE REGISTRY AS WELL AS DESCRIPTORS**, BECAUSE A STORE CONTRACT MAY LAND BEFORE ITS CONSUMING DESCRIPTOR — THE MIRROR IMAGE OF D107's `pending-impl-bindings.json` — AND A REGISTERED CONTRACT IS PRODUCTION SURFACE EITHER WAY. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §3.1 (primary); ref task-contract §3.4 id grammar; `src/domain/intake/header-discovery.ts`; `src/domain/contracts/extraction/`; `src/stores/extraction/`; `tests/scenario/intake-extraction.scenario.test.ts`; `tests/unit/intake-header-discovery.test.ts` |

| **D150** | **LEGACY CHANGE ACCOUNTING IS RETIRED; THE TWO REPOSITORIES TOUCH EACH OTHER IN NEITHER DIRECTION.** OPERATOR DECISION: EVERY WORKFLOW IS REBUILT HERE ANYWAY, SO THERE IS NO ONGOING LEDGER TO RECONCILE — AND D94's GUARD REQUIRED LEGACY's `src`/`tests` WORKTREE TO BE **CLEAN**, WHICH MADE AN UNRELATED IN-FLIGHT EDIT IN THE **PRODUCTION** REPO A RED GATE IN THE REBUILD. THAT IS THE WRONG COUPLING: THE REBUILD MUST NEVER BLOCK ON WHAT PRODUCTION IS DOING TODAY. **WHERE THE INVARIANT WENT:** THE RISK WAS A PORTED SELECTOR OR PAGE-STATE FACT GOING STALE AFTER LEGACY FIXED IT, AND THAT MOVES TO **LIVE RE-VERIFICATION AT EACH WORKFLOW's MIGRATION** — ALREADY MANDATORY AND PRE-AUTHORIZED, AND STRICTLY STRONGER, SINCE A SELECTOR CHECKED AGAINST THE LIVE SYSTEM BEATS ONE DIFFED AGAINST LEGACY's COPY OF IT. ONLY 23 OF 406 PINNED KEYS ARE `ported` TODAY, AND EACH IS LIVE-VERIFIED AT ITS MIGRATION REGARDLESS. `legacy-selector-port-provenance` IS UNAFFECTED BECAUSE IT READS A **FIXED** COMMIT's BLOBS RATHER THAN THE WORKING TREE; THE CAPABILITY/TEST DISPOSITION INVENTORIES LIKEWISE READ LEGACY AS ONE-TIME PLAN COVERAGE AND NEVER BLOCK ON ITS STATE. `config/legacy-change-accounting.json` IS KEPT AS A **FROZEN** HISTORICAL RECORD AND IS READ BY NOTHING. A SIDE DEFECT FELL OUT: `guard-manifest` ASSERTED `retiredBy === "D93"`, A LITERAL THAT COULD ONLY HOLD UNTIL THE SECOND RETIREMENT; IT NOW ASSERTS THE RULE (`/^D\d+$/`). | ref guards-and-tests §3.13 (primary); CLAUDE.md cross-repo rules; `docs/rebuild/guard-inventory.json` (retired entry) |
| **D151** | **AN OCR EXTRACTION IS ADMITTED BY ONE GATE THAT DOES NOT BRANCH ON WHO PRODUCED IT, AND EVERY DECLARED FIELD MUST BE ANSWERED.** OPERATOR REQUIREMENT: A PAGE MAY BE READ BY THE PROVIDER PIPELINE, BY AN AI THE OPERATOR RUNS IN THEIR OWN TERMINAL, OR BY THE OPERATOR — AND ALL THREE MUST YIELD THE SAME RECORD WITH NOBODY SKIPPING ANYTHING. `ExtractionSource` IS A CLOSED UNION RECORDED AS **PROVENANCE, NEVER PERMISSION**; `admitOcrExtraction` CONTAINS NO BRANCH ON IT, SO A ROUTE CANNOT EARN A WEAKER RULE BY BEING MORE CONVENIENT, AND A CANONICAL-BOUND VALUE RUNS THE **SAME** COERCE+SCHEMA AN INTAKE COLUMN RUNS (A MODEL RETURNING `10-4567` FOR AN EID IS REFUSED EXACTLY AS A SPREADSHEET CELL IS). THE INSTRUCTION EVERY ROUTE RECEIVES IS **GENERATED** FROM THE FORM REGISTRY, SO ADDING A FIELD REACHES ALL THREE ROUTES ON THE NEXT RUN AND NO ROUTE CAN SILENTLY STOP ASKING. COMPLETENESS IS MECHANICAL: EVERY DECLARED PATH MUST APPEAR, AN UNDECLARED PATH IS REFUSED, AND THERE IS NO `.optional()` IN THE ANSWER SHAPE — AN OPTIONAL FIELD IS PRECISELY ONE A PRODUCER CAN SKIP IN SILENCE. THE THREE ABSENCES ARE **NOT** INTERCHANGEABLE (`not-on-form` / `blank` / `illegible`); LEGACY COLLAPSED ALL THREE INTO `null` AND REBUILT THE DIFFERENCE BY DIFFING SNAPSHOTS. A READ VALUE ITS FIELD REFUSES BECOMES `unreadable` **WITH ITS RAW TEXT** RATHER THAN VOIDING THE RECORD, SINCE A HUMAN RESOLVES IT AND MUST SEE WHAT THE PRODUCER SAW. `answersFingerprint` DIGESTS WHAT WAS READ WHILE EXCLUDING WHO READ IT, WHEN, AND ITS SELF-REPORTED CONFIDENCE, SO CROSS-ROUTE AGREEMENT IS CHECKABLE RATHER THAN ASSERTED. THE PROVIDER ADAPTER AND THE OPERATOR CLI ARE TRANSPORTS OVER THIS GATE AND MAY ADD NO RULE OF THEIR OWN. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.3 (primary); ref task-contract §3.4 service stores; `src/domain/ocr/`; `tests/unit/ocr-extraction-admission.test.ts` |
| **D152** | **DOC ref data-intake-and-edit-data §3's `parse-pdf-fields` DESCRIBED A CAPABILITY THAT DOES NOT EXIST; THE REAL INTAKE NEED IS RASTERIZATION.** §3 CLAIMED "SAME SHAPE FOR A FIELDED PDF (ONBOARDING's EXTRACTION STEP PORTS HERE)", BUT LEGACY's ONBOARDING `extract.ts` IS PLAYWRIGHT SCRAPING LABELLED FIELDS OFF A **CRM PAGE**, AND **NO LEGACY CODE READS AN ACROFORM ANYWHERE** — LEGACY's ACTUAL PDF USES ARE RASTERIZING PAGES FOR OCR, SPLITTING A PAGE FOR ONBASE, AND TEXT-EXTRACTING KRONOS REPORTS. BUILDING AN ACROFORM READER FOR A FORM NOBODY SUBMITS WOULD BE IMPLEMENTING A MISREMEMBERED REQUIREMENT, SO `extraction/extract-pdf-pages` LANDS INSTEAD: ONE CONTENT-ADDRESSED PDF → ONE CONTENT-ADDRESSED PNG PER PAGE, WHICH IS EXACTLY WHAT `ocr/extract-form-page` CONSUMES. A PAGE WINDOW PAST THE END IS **REFUSED, NEVER CLAMPED** (AN OPERATOR ASKING FOR PAGES 40–50 OF A 44-PAGE SCAN HAS MISCOUNTED, AND RETURNING FIVE WOULD MISNUMBER EVERY DOWNSTREAM `sourcePage`), AND `pageCount` REPORTS THE **DOCUMENT's** COUNT SO A REVIEWER CAN TELL A TWO-PAGE SCAN FROM PAGES 2–3 OF A LONGER ONE. **`pdf-to-img` CHOSEN** (MIT, MAINTAINED 2026-06, ONE DEPENDENCY, WRAPS MOZILLA's `pdfjs-dist` — THE ENGINE LEGACY ALREADY RENDERS WITH); REFUSED: `pdf-lib` (LEGACY's SPLITTER, LAST PUBLISHED 2022-05 AND CANNOT RASTERIZE — NO PDF **WRITER** IS NEEDED ON THIS PATH), `pdfjs-dist` DIRECTLY (HAND-WIRED CANVAS/WORKER PLUMBING), `mupdf` (AGPL-3.0), `pdf-parse`/`unpdf` (TEXT EXTRACTORS; A SCANNED FORM HAS NO TEXT LAYER). TWO FINDINGS FROM A REAL 44-PAGE OPERATOR SCAN ARE ENCODED: THE **JBIG2 WASM DECODER MUST BE WIRED** (XEROX EMITS JBIG2; WITHOUT `wasmUrl` PDFJS FALLS BACK TO ITS JS DECODER AND TAKES ~60% LONGER — 736 ms vs 462 ms FOR FIVE PAGES — AND THE URL IS RESOLVED FROM THE **INSTALLED** `pdfjs-dist` SO IT CANNOT NAME A STALE COPY), AND **PDFJS TRANSFERS ITS INPUT BUFFER**, SO EVERY CALL GETS A FRESH COPY OR A SECOND RENDER THROWS `DataCloneError` ON A DETACHED BUFFER. THE SCENARIO FIXTURE IS A PDF BUILT BY HAND FROM LITERAL PDF OBJECTS, BECAUSE A FIXTURE FROM A PDF **WRITER** WOULD ONLY PROVE THE READER AGREES WITH THAT WRITER. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §3.2 (primary); ref task-contract §3.4; `src/domain/contracts/extraction/extract-pdf-pages.ts`; `src/stores/extraction/tasks/extract-pdf-pages.ts`; `tests/scenario/intake-pdf-pages.scenario.test.ts` |
| **D153** | **THE OCR FORMS ARE LEGACY's, AND PORTING THEM FOUND TWO STRUCTURAL GAPS IN D151's FIRST SHAPE.** OPERATOR CONFIRMATION: THE REBUILD HANDLES THE SAME FORMS, SO THE FIELD SETS ARE PORTED RATHER THAN REDESIGNED — AND READING LEGACY's ACTUAL PROMPTS EXPOSED TWO KINDS OF SKIPPING THE FIRST GATE COULD NOT SEE. **(1) A PAGE CAN CARRY SEVERAL PEOPLE.** AN OATH SIGN-IN SHEET IS A TABLE; A GATE ACCEPTING ONE RECORD PER PAGE WOULD SILENTLY DROP ELEVEN PEOPLE FROM A TWELVE-ROW SHEET AND NO PER-FIELD CHECK WOULD NOTICE. A SUBMISSION NOW CARRIES `records: [{ rowIndex, answers }]`, `recordsPerPage` DECLARES `one` OR `many`, EACH RECORD KEEPS ITS OWN `answersFingerprint`, AND THE PAGE GETS A `pageFingerprint` OVER ALL OF THEM. **(2) A SCANNED STACK IS MIXED.** LEGACY's OWN PROMPTS CLASSIFY EVERY PAGE, BECAUSE OPERATORS FEED IT PILES OF PAPER; WITHOUT IT A PRODUCER HANDED AN EMERGENCY-CONTACT PAGE DURING AN OATH RUN MUST EITHER **INVENT** OATH ANSWERS OR **FAIL** THE PAGE, AND NEITHER IS TRUE. A SUBMISSION DECLARES `pageKind` BEFORE ANY FIELD IS READ, AND A PAGE THAT IS NOT THE EXPECTED FORM CARRIES NO RECORDS AND IS **ADMITTED**. LEGACY's HARD-WON PROMPT KNOWLEDGE MOVES ONTO THE **FORM** AS `extractionNotes` — THE OBSERVED DIGIT TRANSPOSITIONS (`10843962` FOR `10002337`; 8↔9 AND 3↔8), THE OBSERVED LETTER DROPS (`Sharaf` NOT `Shaaf`), "SCAN THE TOP MARGIN BECAUSE ON UPAY585 A STANDALONE EIGHT-DIGIT NUMBER ABOVE THE HEADER IS USUALLY THE REAL ID", "NEVER SKIP THE CONTACT's ADDRESS BECAUSE IT RESEMBLES THE EMPLOYEE's" — AND THE GUARD ASSERTS EVERY NOTE REACHES THE GENERATED BRIEF **VERBATIM**, SO IT CANNOT LAND IN ONE ROUTE's PROMPT STRING AND MISS THE OTHERS. LEGACY's `originallyMissing`, RECONSTRUCTED AFTERWARDS BY DIFFING SNAPSHOTS, IS SUPERSEDED BY THE TYPED ABSENCES RECORDED AT READING TIME BY THE PRODUCER THAT READ THEM. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.4 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.3; `src/domain/ocr/form-registry.ts`; `src/domain/ocr/extraction.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/ocr-producer-parity.test.ts` |
| **D154** | **THE OCR FORM MODEL IS MEASURED AGAINST THE REAL DOCUMENT CORPUS, AND IT CORRECTED D153 TWICE.** OPERATOR, 2026-08-06: THE MULTI-ROW SIGN-IN SHEET IS **USED BY NO WORKFLOW**, AND `hr-automation/data/documents` CONFIRMS IT — EVERY OATH IN THE CORPUS IS A UPAY 586 WITH ONE NAME. SO `recordsPerPage` IS REMOVED AND `record` IS A SINGLE NULLABLE VALUE: AN ARRAY OF AT MOST ONE IS GENERALITY WITH NO USER, AND THIS TREE DOES NOT KEEP THAT. THE CORPUS ALSO CORRECTED THE FIELD KNOWLEDGE — THE **EMPLOYEE ID BOX IS FREQUENTLY BLANK** ON A REAL OATH (NORMAL, AND THE DEPARTMENT NUMBER PRINTED BESIDE IT MUST NOT BE SUBSTITUTED), AND A **SIGNED-BUT-UNWITNESSED** OATH IS COMMON AND MEANINGFUL RATHER THAN AN OCR FAILURE. **THE PAGE-KIND VOCABULARY IS DERIVED, NOT GUESSED:** `i9.pdf` PAGE 1 IS A SECTION 2 FOR ONE PERSON AND PAGE 2 A SECTION 1 FOR A **DIFFERENT** ONE — LOOSE, OUT OF ORDER — AND BOTH READ "PAGE 7/8 OF 9", SO **SEVEN OF EVERY NINE PAGES IN AN I-9 PACKET CARRY NO DATA**. | **D155** | **A TWO-DIGIT YEAR IS RESOLVED AGAINST THE OBSERVATION INSTANT, NOT REFUSED — AND THE FIRST LIVE OCR RUN IS WHAT FORCED IT.** READING `detect-test.pdf` THROUGH ROUTE 2 REPORTED THE STACK CORRECTLY (4 EMERGENCY-CONTACT PAGES FLAGGED **NOT THIS WORKFLOW**, 3 OATH PAGES EXTRACTED, 0 REFUSED) AND EXPOSED ONE DEFECT: PAGE 5 IS DATED `11/3/21` AND `single-oath.pdf` IS DATED `4-23-26` — TWO-DIGIT YEARS, ONE DASH-SEPARATED, AND BOTH ARE THE NORMAL WAY THESE FORMS ARE FILLED IN. THE COERCER REFUSED BOTH **WHILE THE GENERATED BRIEF WAS TELLING EVERY PRODUCER THAT `M/D/YY` AND `M-D-YY` ARE EXPECTED**: THE INSTRUCTION AND THE VALIDATOR CONTRADICTING EACH OTHER, WITH EVERY REAL OATH DATE LANDING `unreadable` ON THE FIRST BATCH. `parseUsOrIsoDate` NOW ACCEPTS `-` AS WELL AS `/` AND TAKES THE 2000s READING UNLESS IT LANDS MORE THAN A YEAR AHEAD OF THE OBSERVATION, OTHERWISE THE 1900s (`21`→2021, `26`→2026, `98`→1998). THAT IS A **DOCUMENTED RULE, NOT A GUESS**, AND IT IS SOUND FOR WHAT THIS SYSTEM READS — A SIGNATURE, HIRE, BIRTH OR SEPARATION DATE IS PAST OR AT MOST AN APPOINTMENT WITHIN THE YEAR, NEVER SEVENTY YEARS EARLY. THE REFERENCE IS THE **OBSERVATION INSTANT, NOT `now`**, CARRIED ON `CellLocation.readAt` RATHER THAN READ FROM A CLOCK INSIDE THE COERCER: THE INTAKE PIPELINE PASSES THE RUN's INSTANT AND THE OCR GATE PASSES THE SUBMISSION's OWN `readAt`, SO RE-ADMITTING A STORED SUBMISSION YEARS LATER RESOLVES ITS DATES EXACTLY AS THE FIRST ADMISSION DID — A CENTURY THAT MOVED UNDER REPLAY WOULD BE THE SILENT WRONG VALUE THIS RULE EXISTS TO AVOID. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.6 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.4; `src/domain/fields/cell-coercion.ts`; `tests/unit/canonical-fields.test.ts` |

THE SCAN IS NOW THIS REPO ONLY, AND THE OWNERSHIP AND COMPUTED-READER CHECKS FILTER `legacy:` REGISTRATIONS OUT RATHER THAN REPORTING DRIFT ON EVERY ONE OF THEM AGAINST A SCAN THAT NEVER LOOKS THERE. **THE CLAIM NARROWS HONESTLY WITH THE SCAN:** THE GUARD NOW SAYS "EVERY ENVIRONMENT READ IN THE REBUILD IS REGISTERED, OWNED AND CLASSIFIED", WHICH IS THE PART THAT GOVERNS WHAT THIS RUNTIME DOES. THE REMAINING `legacy:` ENTRIES ARE A HISTORICAL RECORD; NOTHING VERIFIES THEM AND NOTHING SHOULD EXTEND THEM. | ref guards-and-tests §3.13 (primary); D150; `tests/unit/architecture/runtime-environment-coverage.test.ts` |

PROVENANCE FIRST: `detect-test.pdf` IS `/Creator (VersaLink B7035)` AND `Xerox Scan_08052026141856.pdf` IS `/Creator (VersaLink C7020)` — BOTH **RAW XEROX MFP OUTPUT** — WHILE `multiple-oath.pdf` IS `/Producer (macOS Version 26.1)` AND IS THE ASSEMBLED ONE, SO THE SCANS ARE GROUND TRUTH AND THE TIDY FILE IS NOT. THE TWO SCANS DISAGREE: IN `detect-test.pdf` A PERSON's PAGES **ARE** ADJACENT (ONE ORPHAN EC, THEN THREE EC→OATH PAIRS), WHILE THE 44-PAGE I-9 SCAN's FIRST FOUR PAGES ARE FOUR **DIFFERENT** PEOPLE WITH ALTERNATING SECTIONS — §2 CASEY · §1 CHO · §2 CISNEROS · §1 CHAN — NOT ALPHABETICAL, NOT PAIRED, NO TWO CONSECUTIVE PAGES ONE PERSON's. **ADJACENCY IS THEREFORE A PROPERTY OF HOW A PILE WAS ASSEMBLED, NOT OF SCANNING**, AND GROUPING MUST MATCH BY THE NAME READ OFF EACH PAGE ACROSS THE WHOLE STACK, USING ADJACENCY ONLY AS A TIEBREAKER BETWEEN EQUALLY GOOD MATCHES. A NEIGHBOUR-PAIRING DESIGN WOULD HAVE ATTACHED CASEY's SECTION 1 FROM CHO's PAGE — A REAL I-9 VERIFICATION BOUND TO THE WRONG PERSON. TWO CORPUS PROPERTIES MAKE THE NAME MATCH WORKABLE: AN I-9 §2 CARRIES "EMPLOYEE LAST NAME, FIRST NAME AND MIDDLE INITIAL **FROM SECTION 1**" SO BOTH PAGES NAME THE PERSON INDEPENDENTLY, AND A PAGE WHOSE OWN NAME IS UNREADABLE (`detect-test.pdf` PAGE 2 HAS A STICKY NOTE OVER IT) CAN STILL BE GROUPED FROM ITS PARTNER. AN UNMATCHED §2 IS **FLAGGED, NOT BLOCKED** (OPERATOR, 2026-08-06), AS LEGACY's `orphanSection2` WAS. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.7 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.5; D151/D154 |
| **D158** | **STAGES 1 AND 2 OF THE SCANNER PIPELINE LAND, AND EVERY PAGE IS ACCOUNTED FOR.** `src/domain/ocr/stack.ts` CONSUMES A CLASSIFIED STACK AND GROUPS IT INTO SUBJECTS; `rebuild:cli ocr stack <file> <form-type>` IS THE OPERATOR SURFACE AND ANSWERS "WHICH PAGES DO NOT BELONG IN THIS RUN". **EVERY PAGE LANDS IN EXACTLY ONE BUCKET** — SUBJECT, `unidentified`, `otherForms`, `dataless` — AND `pagesAccounted` MUST EQUAL `pagesClassified`, BECAUSE A PAGE IN NO BUCKET IS A PAGE NOBODY KNOWS WAS SKIPPED. `personMatchKey` IS **EXACT AND NARROW**: LOWERCASED ALPHABETIC TOKENS, SORTED (SO AN I-9 §2 HEADER `Clauldia, Drona A.` MATCHES A §1's SEPARATE BOXES), SINGLE LETTERS DROPPED (A MIDDLE INITIAL IS WRITTEN INCONSISTENTLY AND ITS ABSENCE MUST NOT SPLIT ONE PERSON IN TWO), AND **null BELOW TWO TOKENS** — A SURNAME ALONE WOULD GROUP SIBLINGS — WITH SUCH A PAGE REPORTED `unidentified` RATHER THAN GUESSED AT. THERE IS NO FUZZY SCORING: THAT IS PRECISELY WHERE THIS WOULD START INVENTING PEOPLE. VERIFIED ON BOTH REAL STACKS. **THE SECTIONED-SUBJECT CASE AND ITS ORPHAN OUTCOME ARE DELIBERATELY NOT WRITTEN**: EVERY FORM THAT EXISTS TODAY IS ONE PAGE PER PERSON, SO THAT ARM WOULD BE UNREACHABLE AND UNTESTED, AND IT LANDS WITH THE `i9` FORM UNDER D157's FLAG-NEVER-BLOCK RULE. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.8 (primary); D157; `src/domain/ocr/stack.ts`; `tests/unit/ocr-stack.test.ts` |
| **D159** | **AUTHORITY SCHEMA v11 — INTAKE MANIFESTS AND ADMITTED OCR EXTRACTIONS BECOME DURABLE.** EVERYTHING THE 2h PIPELINES PRODUCED WAS IN-MEMORY: A MANIFEST AND AN EXTRACTION SURVIVED EXACTLY AS LONG AS THE PROCESS DID. BOTH TABLES CARRY THEIR IDENTIFYING FACTS AS **COLUMNS** BESIDE THE RECORD JSON, FOR THE REASON v10 GAVE `failures` A SEARCHABLE FINGERPRINT — "HAS THIS FILE BEEN RUN BEFORE" AND "HAS THIS PAGE ALREADY BEEN READ" ARE CONSTANT OPERATOR QUESTIONS AND A SCAN OVER PARSED JSON IS NOT AN ANSWER. `intake_manifests` IS **IMMUTABLE AND UNDELETABLE BY TRIGGER**: A MANIFEST IS THE RERUN AUTHORITY PROVING WHICH FILE AND MAPPING PRODUCED WHICH ITEMS, AND A RUN HAS ALREADY FANNED OUT AGAINST IT, SO A REWRITE WOULD REWRITE HISTORY SOMEBODY ACTED ON — A RERUN CREATES A NEW MANIFEST AND §5's `rerunDiff` SHOWS THE DIFFERENCE. `ocr_extractions` IS **UNIQUE ON `(source_sha256, source_page, form_type)`** — ONE PAGE, READ ONCE, FOR ONE FORM — SO A SECOND PRODUCER READING THE SAME PAGE WITH A DIFFERENT RESULT IS REFUSED BY NAME RATHER THAN BECOMING A SECOND ROW THAT SILENTLY DOUBLES A PERSON IN THE FAN-OUT; A BYTE-IDENTICAL RE-READ IS IDEMPOTENT SO REPLAY IS SAFE. THE RELEASED v1 TABLE ORDER IS UNTOUCHED (BOTH TABLES JOIN `POST_V1_TABLE_ORDER`), AND v11's LITERAL DIGEST IS APPENDED TO THE PINNED MIGRATION HISTORY. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.9 (primary); ref dashboard-surfaces §2.3; `src/infra/sqlite/migrations.ts`; `src/infra/sqlite/authority-database.ts` |

## Reconciliation round 37 (2026-08-07) — the i9 form: sectioned pages, one uniform record

_phase: 2_

| # | Decision | Owner (normative text) |
|---|---|---|
| **D160** | **THE i9 IS ONE FORM ACROSS TWO LOOSE SECTIONS, AND THE SECTIONED ARMS D158 DEFERRED LAND WITH IT.** A FORM NOW DECLARES ITS DATA-BEARING PAGE KINDS (`OcrFormSpec.pageKinds`) — `formType === pageKind` WAS A COINCIDENCE OF THE ONE-PAGE FORMS — AND `i9` ITSELF IS DELIBERATELY **NOT** A PAGE KIND: A PHYSICAL PAGE IS A SECTION 1 OR A SECTION 2, NEVER "AN I-9", AND A REGISTRY SEAL REFUSES A PAGE KIND CLAIMED BY TWO FORMS. THE RECORD IS **UNIFORM ACROSS BOTH SECTIONS** (LEGACY's OWN PROMPT RULE, MADE STRUCTURAL): EVERY SUBMISSION ANSWERS ALL SIX FIELDS, A FIELD WHOSE BOX LIVES ON THE OTHER SECTION (`OcrFieldSpec.onPageKinds`) IS ANSWERED `not-on-form` — THE ABSENCE REASON BUILT FOR EXACTLY THIS — AND THE GATE **REFUSES** ANYTHING ELSE THERE: A `dateOfBirth` "READ" OFF A SECTION 2 PAGE IS A VALUE FROM NOWHERE, AND `blank`/`illegible` BOTH CLAIM THE BOX EXISTS. MANDATORY BLOCKING IS PER-SECTION. THE FIELD SET AND PROMPT KNOWLEDGE ARE PORTED FROM `legacy:src/services/ocr/forms/i9.ts` AT `4deabc5a` WITH THE ACCURACY RULE's PROVENANCE (LIVE 2026-07-13: A DEGRADED SCAN YIELDED A CONFIDENT FABRICATED SSN+DOB AND A FALSE "NOT FOUND"); NOT PORTED, EACH ON AN EXISTING DECISION: THE `"i9 ssn"` UCRS-419 SHEET (D154 — NO CORPUS EVIDENCE; IT CLASSIFIES `unrelated`, SURFACED BY NAME), THE "NEVER ROUND A 2-DIGIT YEAR" NOTE (D155 RESOLVES THE CENTURY BY DOCUMENTED RULE), AND THE FUZZY LEVENSHTEIN PAIRING (D158 — EXACT MATCH OR A HUMAN). GROUPING GAINS THE SECTIONED SUBJECT: `incomplete` WITH `missingKinds`, **FLAGGED AND NEVER BLOCKED** (D157) — THE ORPHAN §2 IS THIS ARM AS `missingKinds: ["i9-section1"]`, LEGACY's `orphanSection2` MADE GENERAL — AND `duplicate` MEANS A DUPLICATED **KIND**, CARRYING `missingKinds` ORTHOGONALLY SO ONE ARM CANNOT HIDE THE OTHER FACT. §1↔§2 FIELD CORROBORATION NEEDS BOTH EXTRACTIONS IN HAND AND IS DOC 12 EVIDENCE WORK ABOVE THIS LAYER. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.10 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.7/§4.8; `src/domain/ocr/form-registry.ts`; `src/domain/ocr/extraction.ts`; `src/domain/ocr/stack.ts`; `config/canonical-field-inventory.json` |
| **D161** | **ROUTE 1's PRODUCTION ADAPTER IS TRANSPORT PLUS PROVENANCE — THE FIRST REAL `ocr.extract` ADAPTER LANDS, AND IT ADDS NO RULE.** `src/infra/providers/gemini-ocr-extract.ts` (GEMINI, LEGACY's PRIORITY-1 OCR PROVIDER) SENDS THE REGISTRY-GENERATED INSTRUCTION WITH THE PAGE BYTES INLINE AS BASE64 AND RUNS EVERYTHING IT GETS BACK THROUGH THE ONE GATE. IT OWNS EXACTLY TWO THINGS: **TRANSPORT** — THE KEY RIDES THE `x-goog-api-key` HEADER, NEVER THE URL, SO NO MESSAGE OR EVIDENCE LINE CAN CARRY IT; 429 MAPS TO RETRYABLE `rate-exhausted` (THE PROVIDER's OWN `Retry-After`, ELSE THE NEXT FIXED UTC MINUTE — D112's WINDOW SEMANTICS); 401/403 → `adapter-unavailable`; ANYTHING ELSE THROWS SO ADMISSION RECORDS A DURABLE INTERRUPTION; A NON-JSON ANSWER IS `invalid-response` REPORTING SHAPE ONLY, NEVER AN EXCERPT — AND **PROVENANCE** — `formType`/`source`/`readAt`/`sourceSha256`/`sourcePage` ARE STAMPED FROM FACTS THE ADAPTER KNOWS, WHILE `pageKind`/`record` PASS THROUGH VERBATIM AND AN OMITTED KEY STAYS OMITTED SO THE GATE NAMES THE GAP. THE REQUEST ENVELOPE NOW CARRIES THE WHOLE `ArtifactRef` (AN id+sha PAIR NAMED BYTES `open()` COULD NOT RESOLVE); `providerModelChain` JOINED `src/domain/secrets.ts` AS THE MODELS-ENV READER; USAGE COST IS 0 BY DECLARATION UNDER THE FREE-TIER FAMILY AND `ocrExtract`'s ZERO COST CAP. **PROVED LIVE 2026-08-08:** THE REAL MODEL ON THE REAL SCAN's PAGE 13 PRODUCED IN 14.2 s AN ADMITTED `i9-section1` RECORD WHOSE `answersFingerprint` IS **BYTE-IDENTICAL** TO A HUMAN's INDEPENDENT ROUTE-2 READING OF THE SAME PAGE. EXECUTOR/WORKER WIRING (`ctx.providers`/`ctx.artifacts`) IS THE DELIBERATE REMAINDER; THE GUARD ARMS CONFINING `createAdmittedProviderClient` RELAX ONLY WHEN THAT CONSUMER LANDS. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.11 (primary); ref task-contract §3.4; `src/infra/providers/gemini-ocr-extract.ts`; `tests/unit/gemini-ocr-extract-adapter.test.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/provider-capability-boundary.test.ts` |

THE WORKER LOOP FORWARDS BOTH SEAMS VERBATIM; `composeWorkerRuntime` COMPOSES THE WRITER UNDER THE ISOLATION-PINNED ARTIFACT ROOT PLUS ONE GEMINI `ocr.extract` ADAPTER OVER ONE KEY AND ONE MODEL (`OCR_GEMINI_MODEL_ID` WITH PROVENANCE, FRONT-RUNNABLE BY `OCR_GEMINI_MODELS`); NO KEY COMPOSES AN **EMPTY** REGISTRY WITH A LOGGED WARNING, SO A DECLARING RUN IS DENIED `adapter-unavailable` BY ADMISSION — LOUD AND DURABLE, NEVER A GUESSED CLIENT. THE `provider-capability-boundary` ARMS RELAX TO EXPLICIT ALLOWLISTS (MINT+TYPE: KERNEL AND EXECUTOR; IMPORT: EXECUTOR AND COMPOSITION ROOT); EVERY OTHER MODULE STAYS CONFINED, AND THE POOL REMAINS 2h-D. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §4.11 (primary); ref task-contract §3.4; `src/core/run-executor.ts`; `src/core/worker-loop.ts`; `src/server/worker/compose.ts`; `tests/unit/run-executor-capability-wiring.test.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/provider-capability-boundary.test.ts` |

## Reconciliation round 39 (2026-08-09) — the durable intake session, then its enqueue

_phase: 2_

| # | Decision | Owner (normative text) |
|---|---|---|
| **D170** | **THE INTAKE ROUTE CREATES THE SESSION, THE FIVE NON-ENQUEUE ARMS ONLY MUTATE IT, AND ROWS/COLUMNS ARE RE-DERIVED ON EVERY READ.** `POST /api/v1/intake/files?workflow=<id>` TAKES SIZE-CAPPED RAW BYTES, PUBLISHES THEM CONTENT-ADDRESSED, RUNS DISCOVERY THROUGH THE SHARED GRID EDGE AND MINTS THE v12 `intake_sessions` ROW IN `mapping` — THERE IS DELIBERATELY NO OPEN ARM (CAPTURE'S PRECEDENT: CREATION CARRIES **BYTES**, WHICH DO NOT BELONG IN A JOURNALED ENVELOPE), AND A RE-UPLOAD OF THE SAME SOURCE DIGEST **JOINS** ITS OPEN SESSION RATHER THAN FORKING ONE THAT WOULD ENQUEUE THE SAME ROWS TWICE. THE FIVE PLACEHOLDER PAYLOADS **AMEND** (D161/D167's RULE — NO PRODUCER EVER SENT THE OLD SHAPES): `inspect-intake` → `{sheet?, headerRowIndex?}`, `confirm-intake-mapping` → `{targetFieldId, sourceColumnId, headerFingerprint}`, `correct-intake-row` → `{sourceRow, field, correctedValue}`, `exclude-intake-row` → `{sourceRow, reason}`, `validate-intake` PAYLOAD-FREE; `enqueue-intake-manifest` STAYS DEFERRED (2i-E2). THE KERNEL GAINS AN INJECTED DETERMINISTIC `intakeParser` SEAM — ASYNC `prepare` (READ THE ARTIFACT, PARSE THE GRID) PLUS A **PURE SYNCHRONOUS** `parse` (RANK CANDIDATES, SETTLE THE HEADER ROW, DETECT COLUMNS, COMPUTE THE LAYOUT FINGERPRINT), BECAUSE `core` CANNOT REACH A SPREADSHEET PARSER AND ITS AUTHORITY TRANSACTION CANNOT AWAIT; A KEY NOBODY PREPARED **THROWS BY NAME**. `confirmBinding` REMAINS THE ONE MINT AND IS NOW FENCED BY THE LAYOUT ITSELF (A STALE `headerFingerprint` IS REFUSED `schema-mismatch`); ANY MAPPING CHANGE **UNVALIDATES** THE SESSION; A SHEET/HEADER MOVE **DISCARDS** CORRECTIONS AND EXCLUSIONS, WHICH NAMED ROWS UNDER THE OLD HEADER; AND EVERY ARM BUT `inspect-intake` REFUSES WHILE NO HEADER ROW IS SETTLED. `validate-intake` RUNS THE PIPELINE AND RETAINS ONLY `{validatedAt, readAt}` — THE DISPOSITIONS ARE A PURE FUNCTION OF THE RETAINED INPUTS PLUS THAT `readAt`, SO THE WIRE RE-DERIVES THEM AND NOTHING CAN DRIFT. THE PROJECTION IS DECLARED ON THE WORKFLOW's **SERVER MODULE**, NOT ITS DESCRIPTOR (IT IS NOT RUN-CONTRACT IDENTITY, AND FOLDING IT INTO `contractFingerprint` WOULD RE-STAMP EVERY EXISTING RUN); NO WORKFLOW DECLARES ONE YET — person-lookup's INPUT IS A UNION — SO THE ROUTE AND EVERY ARM REFUSE BY NAME UNTIL 2i-E3. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.7 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.2/§2.3/§5.0; `src/domain/intake/parse.ts`; `src/domain/intake/wire.ts`; `src/domain/commands.ts`; `src/core/command-kernel.ts`; `src/server/intake/intake-parser.ts`; `src/server/dashboard/commands/intake-service.ts`; `tests/unit/intake-command-arms.test.ts`; `tests/unit/server/intake-surface.test.ts` |
| **D171** | **THE INTAKE ENQUEUE ACTIVATES — READINESS IS JUDGED ON THE MANIFEST IT WOULD WRITE, AND MANIFEST + N RUNS + THE SESSION CAS COMMIT AS ONE.** `enqueue-intake-manifest` AMENDS ITS PLACEHOLDER TARGET (AN `intakeManifestId` CANNOT EXIST WHEN THE COMMAND THAT MINTS IT IS ISSUED) TO `{intakeSessionId, expectedVersion}` PLUS AN EXPLICIT `{dryRun}`, AND IS THE **ONE** INTAKE ARM THAT CASes — IF THE SESSION MOVED SINCE THE OPERATOR READ THE REJECT LIST THEY WOULD BE ENQUEUEING A PLAN THEY NEVER SAW. `deriveIntakeEnqueuePlan` RE-RUNS VALIDATION AGAINST THE **RETAINED** `readAt`, BUILDS THE MANIFEST AND RETURNS IT BESIDE `enqueueReadiness(manifest)`, SO NO SECOND COUNT CAN DISAGREE WITH THE ROW THE OPERATOR IS HANDED; A CORRECTION's `original` IS READ BACK FROM THE SOURCE CELL AND REDACTED BY THE CANONICAL FIELD's OWN DECLARED TRANSFORM, AND AN INCONSISTENT SESSION/LAYOUT **THROWS** RATHER THAN PUTTING A PLAUSIBLE BLANK IN AN IMMUTABLE MANIFEST. `deriveMappingSnapshotHash` IS THE IDENTITY OF THE **DECISIONS** (WORKFLOW, SHEET, HEADER ROW, LAYOUT FINGERPRINT, BINDINGS, CONSTANTS — SORTED; `confirmedAt`, CORRECTIONS AND EXCLUSIONS DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED). THE MANIFEST MUST NAME ITS FILE, SO THE UPLOAD ROUTE REQUIRES `?filename=` AND THE SESSION RETAINS `sourceName` — OPTIONAL ON THE RECORD (D133: READ FORWARD, NEVER BACK-FILLED), REQUIRED AT THE ROUTE, REFUSED BY NAME AT ENQUEUE. `start-run` AND THIS ARM NOW SHARE **ONE** `fanOutQueuedRuns`: THE ENQUEUE POLICY IS EVALUATED PER ITEM AGAINST LIVE AUTHORITY AND A `reject-active` COLLISION REFUSES THE WHOLE COMMAND. AN INTAKE RUN IS SERVER-STAMPED `shape:"single"` / `priority:"bulk"`, ITS CANONICAL INPUT RE-READ FROM THE MANIFEST THROUGH THE DESCRIPTOR's CURRENT CANONICAL SCHEMA (D62 — NEVER ITS INGRESS PARSER), AND ITS CONFIG IDENTITY COMES FROM A NEW INJECTED `configIdentity` SEAM THAT THE ARM **PROVES** AGAINST THE STORED `config_snapshots` ROW BEFORE STAMPING A SINGLE ENVELOPE. MANIFEST INSERT + EVERY RUN + THE SESSION CAS TO `enqueued` RUN IN THE ONE OUTER TRANSACTION, SO PARTIAL FAN-OUT IS UNREPRESENTABLE. **DEVIATION, RECORDED:** DOC ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5's `operation` COORDINATOR + `operation-member` CHILDREN HAVE NO RUNTIME (NOTHING MINTS AN `operation` RUN), SO INTAKE FANS OUT N INDEPENDENT `single` RUNS AND THE MANIFEST IS THE JOIN — WHICH IS WHAT §5 ALREADY RELIES ON TO MAKE "42 ROWS RAN" CHECKABLE. THE COMMAND SERVICE EMITS ONE `run.queued` SPAN PER RUN, JOINED TO THE MANIFEST BY `itemId` AND TITLED BY THE WORKFLOW's OWN PRESENTATION; A COUNT MISMATCH THROWS. THE SESSION WIRE SERVES `rerun {manifestId, createdAt, totals, diff}` WHENEVER THESE EXACT BYTES ALREADY HAVE A MANIFEST FOR THIS WORKFLOW (§7 #11), ON UPLOAD AND ON GET ALIKE. FINALLY, `intakeProjectionRegistry` NOW REFUSES A PROJECTION BUILT OVER A **COPY** OF ITS DESCRIPTOR's INPUT SCHEMA — THE SAME IDENTITY-NOT-EQUIVALENCE ARGUMENT `defineIntakeProjection` MAKES PER TARGET FIELD, APPLIED TO THE WHOLE INPUT. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5.2 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5/§2.7; `src/domain/intake/enqueue.ts`; `src/domain/intake/mapping.ts`; `src/domain/intake/session.ts`; `src/domain/intake/wire.ts`; `src/domain/commands.ts`; `src/core/command-kernel.ts`; `src/control/workflow-registry.ts`; `src/server/dashboard/commands/command-service.ts`; `src/server/dashboard/commands/intake-service.ts`; `tests/unit/intake-command-arms.test.ts`; `tests/unit/server/intake-surface.test.ts` |

| **D172** | **THE FIRST REAL INTAKE PROJECTION LANDS ON person-lookup, AND A UNION INPUT SCHEMA IS TRAVERSED ON ONE RULE.** `resolveSchemaPath` REFUSED UNIONS OUTRIGHT, WHICH MADE THE WHOLE UNION-INPUT CLASS OF WORKFLOWS UNMAPPABLE; BOTH TEMPTING WORKAROUNDS ARE SILENT-WRONG-VALUE BUGS (RESOLVING AGAINST *THE FIRST ARM THAT HAS THE KEY* PICKS ONE SHAPE's SCHEMA FOR A VALUE THE OTHER SHAPE PARSES; FLATTENING INVENTS A SCHEMA THE WORKFLOW NEVER DECLARED). THE PATH NOW RESOLVES IN **EVERY** MEMBER AND MUST RESOLVE TO THE **SAME SCHEMA OBJECT** WHEREVER IT RESOLVES — TWO ARMS SPELLING `z.string().min(1)` TWICE ARE TWO OBJECTS, SO `divergent-across-union` REFUSES BY NAME AND SAYS HOW MANY ARMS DISAGREED; A MEMBER LACKING THE PATH MAKES THE TARGET **OMISSIBLE**, SO `required` MEANS "EVERY VALID INPUT MUST SUPPLY THIS" — THE ONLY READING UNDER WHICH §5's `unmapped-required` REJECTION IS TRUE; AND WHEN NO MEMBER RESOLVES IT THE FAILURE REPORTED IS THE ONE THAT WALKED **FURTHEST**, SO THE MESSAGE NAMES A REAL SHAPE'S FIELDS RATHER THAN "A UNION". A UNION AT THE **LEAF** IS A VALUE SHAPE, NOT A FORK, AND RESOLVES AS ITSELF. `person-lookup` THEREFORE SHARES ONE `PersonNameSchema` — WHICH **IS** `CANONICAL_FIELDS.fullName.schema`, SINCE §1's INVARIANT IS PROVED BY OBJECT IDENTITY — ACROSS BOTH INPUT ARMS AND THE CANONICAL VIEW, AND ITS SERVER MODULE DECLARES `{emplId → eid, name → fullName}` AND DELIBERATELY NOTHING ELSE: `keepNonHdh`/`includeCrmDates` ARE RECORDED `execution-option` IN THE CANONICAL SCAN BECAUSE THEY DESCRIBE THE **RUN**, AND BINDING A COLUMN TO ONE WOULD LET A FILE DECIDE WHETHER THIS SYSTEM READS CRM. NEITHER TARGET IS `required` — THAT IS THE UNION TELLING THE TRUTH — AND THE COMBINATION IS ENFORCED ONE LEVEL DOWN BY §5 PARSING EVERY ASSEMBLED ROW THROUGH THIS EXACT SCHEMA. THE DESCRIPTOR's SOURCE CLOSURE MOVED, SO `contractFingerprint` AND THE PARITY `wf` STAMP MOVED WITH IT. **RESIDUAL:** ZOD REPORTS A FAILED NON-DISCRIMINATED UNION AS A ROOT `Invalid input`, SO SUCH A ROW REJECTS LEGIBLY AS `workflow-schema` WITHOUT NAMING WHICH IDENTIFIER WAS MISSING. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.8 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §1/§2.7; `src/validation/schema-path.ts`; `src/domain/intake/projection.ts`; `src/workflows/person-lookup/descriptor.ts`; `src/workflows/person-lookup/module.ts`; `tests/unit/intake-mapping.test.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/descriptor-coverage.test.ts` |
| **D173** | **THE INTAKE WORKSPACE LANDS, AND PROPOSED-AGAINST-DECIDED IS A PLACE RATHER THAN A COLOUR.** A DECIDED BINDING IS A SOLID CONTROL IN THE ROW'S OWN `Bound column` CELL WITH THE FILE'S REAL SAMPLE VALUES BENEATH IT (THE OPERATOR CONFIRMS WHAT A COLUMN **HOLDS**, NOT WHAT IT IS CALLED); A SUGGESTION IS RECESSED, DASHED, IN A **DIFFERENT COLUMN**, AND CARRIES AN EXPLICIT VERB PLUS THE SCORER'S OWN REASONS — AN `ambiguous` PROPOSAL STATES ITS AMBIGUITY AND GETS NO VISUAL PREFERENCE. TWO STATES NEVER IN THE SAME PLACE CANNOT BE CONFUSED; A TONE WOULD HAVE HAD TO BE LEARNED. GRID ROWS ARE THE **WORKFLOW's DECLARED TARGETS**, NEVER THE FILE'S COLUMNS, SO AN UNMAPPED REQUIRED FIELD IS VISIBLE BY CONSTRUCTION. IT IS A **WORKSPACE AT `?intake=<sessionId>`, NOT A DIALOG** — THE SESSION IS A DURABLE ROW, SO TRAPPING IT IN A MODAL WOULD TRAP A TASK WHOSE STATE IS ALREADY SAFE; THE TAKEOVER HIDES THE QUEUE RATHER THAN DIMMING IT, AND THE OUTCOME BAND IS STICKY SO A DISABLED `Run` NEVER LOSES ITS REASON OFF-SCREEN. READINESS IS **DERIVED FOR COURTESY AND JUDGED BY THE KERNEL**: THE BAND NAMES THE BLOCKERS ON THE SERVED WIRE, AND `enqueueReadiness`'s REFUSAL RENDERS VERBATIM. STALENESS NEEDS NO CLIENT ARITHMETIC — EVERY MAPPING ARM SETS `validation:{state:"none"}`. A SHEET/HEADER CHANGE CONFIRMS FIRST **WITH THE COUNTS IT WILL DISCARD**, AND THE SURFACE TRACKS WHICH COMMAND IS IN FLIGHT SO THE PRESSED CONTROL ALONE SPINS. NEW `Dropzone` DS PRIMITIVE (A REAL `<input type="file">` IN A `<label>`; IT **NEVER FILTERS A DROPPED FILE** — THE SERVER REFUSES BY NAME; DRAG DEPTH IS A COUNTER, NOT A BOOLEAN). THE TRANSPORT GREW `requestJsonResponse` BECAUSE THE UPLOAD'S **STATUS IS THE ANSWER** (`201` CREATED / `200` JOINED / `422` REFUSED; ANY OTHER STATUS **THROWS** RATHER THAN BEING CALLED A REFUSAL). FOUND AND FIXED: `Banner` FORWARDS NO UNKNOWN PROPS, SO A SPREAD `data-demo-enter` TYPE-CHECKS AND IS DROPPED AT RUNTIME — ARRIVING SURFACES WRAP INSTEAD. **DEFERRED WITH ITS REASON:** NO ARM SETS A RUN-WIDE CONSTANT AND NONE REMOVES A BINDING, SO CONSTANTS RENDER READ-ONLY AND THE COLUMN CONTROL OFFERS NO "UNBIND" — EITHER WOULD BE A CONTROL THAT SILENTLY DOES NOTHING. | ref dashboard-surfaces §11 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §2.2/§2.5/§5.2; `src/dashboard/surfaces/intake.tsx`; `src/dashboard/ds/primitives-form.tsx`; `src/dashboard/client/queries.ts`; `src/dashboard/client/transport.ts`; `src/dashboard/app/dashboard-app.tsx`; `tests/unit/dashboard/intake-client.test.ts`; `tests/parity/helpers/fixture-intake.ts` |

| **D174** | **THE CAPTURE TOKEN IS A CREDENTIAL THAT NEVER ENTERS A URL THE SERVER SEES, THE PHONE'S ROUTE UNION IS CLOSED AND DENY-BY-DEFAULT, AND THE FINALIZE HANDOFF'S IDENTITIES ARE DERIVED BECAUSE D167 PROMISED THEY WOULD BE.** 32 CSPRNG BYTES AS base64url (NOT A UUID — SIX FIXED VERSION/VARIANT BITS ARE THE WRONG SHAPE FOR A BEARER SECRET) RIDE THE QR PAYLOAD'S **URL FRAGMENT**, WHICH NO BROWSER TRANSMITS AND NO PROXY LOG CAN RECORD, AND REACH THE API IN THE `X-Capture-Token` **HEADER** — NEVER A PATH SEGMENT, NEVER A QUERY VALUE (DOC 11 BANS SECRET-BEARING URLs); ONLY A DOMAIN-SEPARATED DIGEST IS DURABLE. ENUMERATION IS FAIL-CLOSED WITH **ONE** ANSWER: A MALFORMED TOKEN, AN UNKNOWN DIGEST, AN EXPIRED SESSION AND A DISCARDED ONE PRODUCE THE SAME STATUS AND THE SAME SENTENCE, BECAUSE A PHONE ON A PUBLIC ORIGIN THAT COULD TELL "NO SUCH SESSION" FROM "NOT YOURS" IS AN EXISTENCE ORACLE; EVERY OTHER REFUSAL NAMES ITSELF, SINCE BY THEN THE CALLER HAS **PROVEN** IT HOLDS THE TOKEN. `CAPTURE_PHONE_ROUTES` IS DOC ref operator-trust §7's EXCEPTION WRITTEN DOWN ONCE AND DERIVED TWICE (REGISTRATION AND INGRESS ALLOWLIST); A REQUEST ON THE INGRESS **Host** IS REFUSED WITH A BODY **BYTE-IDENTICAL TO AN UNROUTED PATH**, AND A `:param` MATCHES EXACTLY ONE SEGMENT SO NO PATH WALKS OUT OF THE PREFIX. INGRESS RESOLUTION IS A **NAMED CONFIGURATION RESULT, NEVER A FALLBACK**: `on-demand-ngrok` REFUSES `ingress-not-built` EVEN WITH ITS CREDENTIAL, BECAUSE A LOOPBACK QR SCANS PERFECTLY AND THEN FAILS ON THE PHONE WITH A NETWORK ERROR THAT EXPLAINS NOTHING; THE SESSION STAMPS `{mode, originDigest}` THROUGH AN **OPTIONAL** LEAF (D133 — READ FORWARD, NEVER BACK-FILLED). EXPIRY IS A DURABLE TRANSITION ON `open` ALONE, AND THE ACTIVE BUDGET IS SET **ONCE** AT FIRST TOUCH — A SLIDING BUDGET WOULD MAKE EVERY STATUS POLL A WRITE. THE BUNDLER IS A DETERMINISTIC DEPENDENCY-FREE PDF WRITER THAT RE-VERIFIES EACH REF'S DIGEST, EMBEDS JPEG (`/DCTDecode`) AND PNG (`/FlateDecode` + `/Predictor 15`) WITH **NO RE-ENCODE**, REFUSES BY NAME WHAT `DCTDecode` CANNOT DECODE OR WHAT WOULD NEED REAL DECODING (PROGRESSIVE, 12-BIT, PALETTE/ALPHA/16-BIT/INTERLACED, `tRNS`), CORRECTS ADOBE APP14 CMYK INVERSION, AND EMITS NO DATE AND A DIGEST-FOLDED `/ID` — WHICH IS WHAT MAKES D167's CONVERGENCE-BY-DIGEST TRUE RATHER THAN HOPED FOR. THE HANDOFF'S MANIFEST AND RUN IDS ARE `deriveStableUuid`d FROM THE OUTBOX's `stableKey` BECAUSE `ids.uuid()` CANNOT KEEP "A REPLAY RETURNS THE SAME IDENTITIES"; THE MANIFEST IS A REAL `IntakePlanManifest` WITH §5.1's CAPTURE-ONLY FACTS IN A NEW **OPTIONAL** `capture` BLOCK, AND `fanOutQueuedRuns` MOVED TO `src/core/run-fan-out.ts` WITH ITS REFUSAL AS A CALLBACK SO CAPTURE IS A THIRD CALLER OF D171's ONE FAN-OUT RATHER THAN A COPY. **NO REGISTERED WORKFLOW CAN DECLARE A CAPTURE INTAKE BINDING** — NEITHER AN EMPLOYEE ID NOR A DOCUMENT NUMBER IS DERIVABLE FROM A PHOTOGRAPH WITHOUT AN OCR-BACKED WORKFLOW THAT DOES NOT EXIST — SO THE HANDOFF **REFUSES BY NAME** INTO DURABLE `failed` + RETRY FINALIZE, THE SHAPE `INTAKE_PROJECTIONS` HAD BETWEEN D170 AND D172. `capture-durability-and-scope` ACTIVATES. | ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5.1 (primary); ref clock-config-secrets §capture ingress; ref operator-trust §7; `src/domain/capture-surface.ts`; `src/domain/capture-intake.ts`; `src/domain/capture.ts`; `src/domain/intake/manifest.ts`; `src/core/capture-intake-handoff.ts`; `src/core/run-fan-out.ts`; `src/infra/capture/pdf-bundler.ts`; `src/server/capture/compose.ts`; `src/server/dashboard/commands/capture-service.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/capture-durability-and-scope.test.ts` |
| **D175** | **THE PHONE IS A SEPARATE BUNDLE, ONE PAGE WHOSE BOTTOM BAR CHANGES VERB, AND ITS FAILURES ARE THE SERVER'S OWN SENTENCES.** THE USE SCENE PICKS THE THEME: THE OPERATOR DASHBOARD IS DARK BECAUSE IT LIVES ON A DESK, AND THE PHONE IS **LIGHT** BECAUSE ITS SCENE IS SOMEBODY STANDING AT A CABINET UNDER A WINDOW — SPENDING NO NEW COLOUR, SINCE `tokens.css` ALREADY SHIPS BOTH THEMES. IT IS A **SEPARATE VITE ENTRY** (`capture.html` OVER `src/dashboard/phone/`), NOT A ROUTE IN THE OPERATOR SPA, BECAUSE SERVING THE OPERATOR BUNDLE OVER A PUBLIC TUNNEL SHIPS THE WHOLE OPERATOR UI TO THE PHONE ORIGIN AND NO ROUTE FILTER CAN UN-SEND BYTES; `build.manifest` IS EMITTED SO THE INGRESS ALLOWLIST IS **GENERATED** FROM THAT ENTRY'S OWN ASSET CLOSURE, `dashboard-wire-boundary` NOW WALKS **EVERY** `*.html` ENTRY (A SECOND ENTRY WOULD OTHERWISE ESCAPE D120's NODE-BUILT-IN CHECK), AND THE PHONE CLOSURE MAY REACH NO OPERATOR SURFACE/CLIENT/APP MODULE. CAPTURE → REVIEW/REORDER → FINALIZE ARE THREE STATES OF **ONE PAGE**, NOT A WIZARD: THE TASK IS SHORT, THE DURABLE SESSION MEANS A RELOAD LANDS WHERE THE OPERATOR WAS, AND A STEP INDICATOR WOULD NARRATE A SEQUENCE THE PINNED THUMB-ZONE BAR ALREADY STATES. **ORDER IS MEANING**, SO EVERY TILE CARRIES ITS PAGE NUMBER AS A LARGE NUMERAL AND REORDERING IS EXPLICIT ▲/▼ CONTROLS — A DRAG TARGET INSIDE A SCROLLING TOUCH LIST CANNOT BE HALF-PERFORMED WITH A KEYBOARD AND ANNOUNCES NOTHING. PHOTOGRAPHS COME FROM A REAL `<input capture="environment">` IN A `<label>` (D173's PATTERN): BUILDING A VIEWFINDER WOULD RE-IMPLEMENT FOCUS/EXPOSURE/ORIENTATION WORSE THAN THE PHONE, ON THE ONE SURFACE WHERE A BAD PHOTOGRAPH IS THE WHOLE FAILURE. THE ERROR REGION IS ALWAYS IN THE DOM WITH RESERVED HEIGHT SO A REFUSAL NEVER JUMPS THE LAYOUT UNDER A MOVING THUMB, IS `role="alert"`, AND RENDERS THE SERVER'S MESSAGE **VERBATIM** — THE PHONE COMPOSES NO PROSE AND SWALLOWS NO CATCH. THE CAMERA STAYS PRIMARY EVEN AFTER THE FIRST PHOTOGRAPH BECAUSE PROMOTING Review WOULD PUT Review AND Send BOTH "PRIMARY, ON THE RIGHT" AND A SECOND TAP FROM A TRAVELLING THUMB WOULD FINALIZE THE BUNDLE. MOTION IS THE HOUSE LANGUAGE (`--ds-dur-enter`/`--ds-travel-md`, ONE AUTHORED MOMENT), NOT AN INVENTED `--ds-phone-*` DIALECT THAT WOULD NEED ITS OWN REDUCED-MOTION BLOCK; PRESS FEEDBACK IS A 3% SCALE, NOT A 1px TRANSLATE, BECAUSE A FINGER **COVERS** A 48px CONTROL. RED IS RESERVED FOR Discard. THE OPERATOR'S HALF IS ONE SMALL CONTROL RENDERING THE RETURNED `phoneUrl` AS AN INLINE-SVG QR FROM A **PURE** ENCODER (`src/domain/qr-code.ts`, ISO/IEC 18004, VERSIONS 1–40, NO DEPENDENCY), SO THE PAYLOAD NEVER LEAVES THE PROCESS; THE RAW TOKEN IS RENDERED AND NEVER STORED, AND A REFUSED INGRESS RENDERS ITS `message` + `remediation` IN PLACE OF THE QR. **15 PARITY REFERENCES ADDED** AT ONE PHONE VIEWPORT IN THEIR OWN BLOCK (THE OPERATOR CORPUS IS NOT MULTIPLIED); THE 12 THAT MOVED ARE THE `intake-upload` TRIPLES THE QR CONTROL LIVES ON, AND NOTHING MOVED SILENTLY. | ref dashboard-surfaces §12 (primary); ref data-intake-and-edit-data §5.1; ref frontend-backend-integration §7.1; `src/dashboard/phone/**`; `src/dashboard/capture.html`; `src/dashboard/surfaces/capture-start.tsx`; `src/domain/qr-code.ts`; `vite.dashboard.config.ts`; `tests/parity/helpers/fixture-capture.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/dashboard-wire-boundary.test.ts` |

## Reconciliation round 42 (2026-08-09) — the explorer reads the registry, and the catalog gets a surface

_phase: 2_

| # | Decision | Owner (normative text) |
|---|---|---|
| **D176** | **THE READ-ONLY WORKFLOW EXPLORER LANDS AS A PROJECTION OF THE COMPOSITION ROOT, AND A READ-ONLY EXPLORER MUST NOT LOOK LIKE AN EDITOR.** IT DERIVES FROM `WORKFLOW_MODULES` PLUS EACH DESCRIPTOR'S FACTORY-SEALED PROJECTIONS, THE BUILD MANIFEST AND THE ON-DISK INVENTORIES — NO LITERAL NODE LIST, NO PER-WORKFLOW SWITCH, NO HAND-MAINTAINED TABLE — SO "THIS IS THE AS-BUILT GRAPH" IS CHECKABLE RATHER THAN A CAPTION. **SOURCE LINKS RESOLVE, THEY NEVER INTERPOLATE:** EVERY PATH IS A CANDIDATE LOOKED UP IN A REAL LISTING (THE MANIFEST'S OWN FINGERPRINTED CLOSURE FOR A CONTRACT AND ITS STORE IMPL, THE SCENARIO DIRECTORY FOR A SCENARIO) AND A MISS IS `unresolved` CARRYING THE PATH IT TRIED — PRINTING THE CONVENTIONAL PATH BECAUSE IT USUALLY HOLDS IS THE BANNED PLAUSIBLE-WRONG VALUE. **UI LINKS ARE REVERSED OUT OF THE CATALOG'S OWN `taskUses`**, NEVER RE-DERIVED, SO THERE IS NO SECOND ANSWER TO DISAGREE WITH `semantic-ui-registry`. **A CLOSURE IS REPORTED AS ITS PRESENCE, NEVER DECOMPILED:** `inputSubject` SAYS *fixed* OR *derived* RATHER THAN BEING EVALUATED AGAINST A FABRICATED INPUT, AND AN `Infinity` FRESHNESS BOUND CROSSES THE WIRE AS THE WORD BECAUSE `JSON.stringify` TURNS IT INTO `null`, WHICH READS AS A MISSING BOUND. THE SURFACE **REFUSES THE WORKFLOW-BUILDER CANVAS** — A MUTATION AFFORDANCE DRAWN OVER A GRAPH NOBODY MAY MUTATE — AND SPENDS ITS BUDGET ON IDENTIFIERS: A STRUCTURE RAIL THAT INDENTS A CONTROLLER'S DOWNSTREAM NODES (SO A BRANCH CASE READS "INSTEAD OF" AND A PARALLEL BRANCH "ALONGSIDE", WHICH `dependsOn` CANNOT EXPRESS) BESIDE A RECORD COLUMN CARRYING ONE UNINTERRUPTED READ. ONLY `transaction` IS TINTED, BECAUSE IT IS THE ONE NODE KIND THAT CAN CHANGE A LIVE HR SYSTEM. **TWO NAMED EXCLUSIONS:** §5.2'S LIVE-RUN OVERLAY (DOC 03'S TIMELINE ALREADY SERVES IT PER RUN; JOINING THE TWO IS ITS OWN WIRE) AND SCENARIO COVERAGE **CLAIMS** (THEY LIVE IN THE EXECUTABLE CORPUS, `scenario-coverage` AUDITS THEM BOTH WAYS, AND `src/` IMPORTING A TEST MODULE WOULD PUT A SECOND COPY IN THE PRODUCT — THE NODE'S OWN DERIVED COVERAGE POINTS ARE SERVED INSTEAD). PHASE A IS READ-ONLY **STRUCTURALLY**: NO COMMAND ENDPOINT, NO COMMAND CLIENT, NO NON-GET REQUEST, `app.get` WITH NO MUTATING COUNTERPART. `workflow-editor-compile-integrity` ACTIVATES **SCOPED**, HOLDING DOC ref guards-and-tests §3.10'S ROUND-TRIP SENTENCE OVER THE REAL REGISTRY IN BOTH DIRECTIONS PLUS D79a'S NO-DSL TRIM; §5.3/§5.4'S PHASE-B DRAFT/APPLY ARMS HAVE NO CODE AND THE INVENTORY ENTRY SAYS SO — A GUARD WHOSE INVARIANT OVERSTATED ITS COVERAGE WOULD BE WORSE THAN A PLANNED ONE. | ref operator-trust §5.7 (primary); ref dashboard-surfaces §13; ref guards-and-tests §3.10; `src/domain/dashboard/explorer.ts`; `src/server/dashboard/queries/explorer.ts`; `src/dashboard/surfaces/explorer.tsx`; `src/dashboard/app/url-state.ts`; `tests/unit/architecture/workflow-editor-compile-integrity.test.ts`; `tests/unit/server/explorer-query.test.ts`; `tests/parity/helpers/fixture-explorer.ts` |
| **D177** | **THE GENERATED UI CATALOG GETS A SURFACE, AND IT SERVES THE COMMITTED ARTIFACT RATHER THAN REBUILDING IT.** §1.3'S CATALOG WAS REACHABLE ONLY FROM `npm run selector:search` AND TWO MARKDOWN PROJECTIONS, WHILE THE OPERATOR WHO NEEDS IT MOST IS ALREADY IN THE DASHBOARD LOOKING AT A FAILED RUN. THE ROUTE OPENS `generated/ui-catalog.json` **READ-ONLY**, PARSES IT, AND THEN ASSERTS THROUGH THE DRIVERS' OWN `assertSafeUiCatalogFresh` THAT THE BYTES STILL EQUAL WHAT THE REGISTRIES COMPOSE; A STALE ARTIFACT **THROWS WITH THE GENERATOR'S OWN REMEDIATION** RATHER THAN BEING SERVED, BECAUSE A CATALOG THAT DISAGREES WITH THE DRIVERS DESCRIBES SELECTORS THE SYSTEM DOES NOT USE AND ACTING ON IT IS ACTING ON FICTION. IT **NEVER REGENERATES** — `npm run ui:catalog` IS THE ONE WRITER, AND A SURFACE THAT REGENERATED ON READ WOULD SILENTLY HEAL A STALE CHECKOUT MID-SESSION, THE EXACT 2026-08-03 TRACKED-ARTIFACT DEFECT. **PROVENANCE SITS BESIDE THE ID, NOT BEHIND IT:** §10 SAYS THE REGISTRY CANNOT MAKE A STALE LIVE VERIFICATION CURRENT, SO EVERY ROW CARRIES ITS `verifiedAt` AND BOTH EVIDENCE REFS AT THE SAME WEIGHT AS ITS ID RATHER THAN IN A DISCLOSURE A HURRIED READER SKIPS. SEARCH IS DEFINED ONCE (`matchesUiCatalogQuery`) OVER ID/LABEL/SYSTEM/KIND/`searchTerms`, CASE-INSENSITIVE, AND **EVERY TERM MUST HIT** — AN OPERATOR NARROWING A 49-ENTRY LIST EXPECTS THE SECOND WORD TO FILTER RATHER THAN WIDEN. ENTRIES GROUP BY SYSTEM BECAUSE THE SYSTEMS **ARE** THE REGISTRIES' TOP-LEVEL PARTITION AND A CRM ELEMENT BETWEEN TWO KUALI ONES IS NOISE TO SOMEBODY DEBUGGING KUALI. | ref operator-trust §5.8 (primary); ref operator-trust §1.3; ref dashboard-surfaces §13; `src/server/dashboard/queries/ui-catalog.ts`; `src/dashboard/surfaces/ui-catalog.tsx`; `src/domain/dashboard/explorer.ts`; `tests/unit/dashboard/explorer-client.test.ts` |

AN ARCHIVED RUN HOLDS ITS **FINAL PROJECTED ROW** PLUS DETAIL, RECEIPT, FAILURE AND EVIDENCE **POINTERS**, TAKEN THROUGH AN `ArchiveSnapshotSource` SEAM OVER THE SAME PROJECTION THE LIVE SURFACES RENDER, SO THE FROZEN BYTES ARE THE BYTES THE OPERATOR LAST SAW; THE WORKFLOW **LABEL IS FROZEN IN** BECAUSE RESOLVING IT LATER WOULD BE A DESCRIPTOR READ ON THE ARCHIVED PATH AND FALLING BACK TO THE ID WOULD SUBSTITUTE A PLAUSIBLE VALUE. **`apply` RE-DERIVES CANDIDATES AND BLOCKERS FROM AUTHORITY INSIDE ITS OWN TRANSACTION**, NEVER FROM THE PLAN'S COPY — A RUN THAT WENT QUEUED BETWEEN REVIEW AND APPLY IS EXACTLY THE POSSIBLE-SUBMIT THE REFUSAL EXISTS TO KEEP OUT — AND REFUSES `active-version-blockers` NAMING EVERY BLOCKER, PLUS REGISTRY DRIFT AND AN APP BUILD THAT MOVED; A REFUSED APPLY LEAVES THE PLAN `planned`, SO A REFUSAL IS NOT A SPENT PLAN. `plan` IS MINTED **EVEN WHEN BLOCKED**, BECAUSE THE LIST IS WHAT THE OPERATOR NEEDS TO CLEAR IT. `relaunch` CITES THE CONTENT DIGEST AND REPLANS FROM THE ARCHIVED **RAW** SUBMISSION THROUGH TODAY'S SCHEMAS — NEVER THE ARCHIVED CANONICAL VALUE, WHICH A MOVED SCHEMA VALIDATED — AND IS NOT A RETRY. ARCHIVED ROWS LEAVE EVERY LIVE READ **ONCE, INSIDE THE PROJECTION**, BESIDE HIDDEN ROWS AND MEMBER CONTAINMENT. THE RESTORE PATH WAS COMPLETE, TESTED AND **UNREACHABLE**. THE FIX IS **ONE LOCK FILE PER ROLE**: THE DASHBOARD (`server`) AND WORKER (`workers`) COMPOSITIONS EACH TAKE THEIR OWN FILE BEFORE THE AUTHORITY DATABASE OPENS AND RELEASE IT AFTER THE HANDLE CLOSES, AND THE SNAPSHOT IS **COMPOSED AT OBSERVATION TIME** FROM BOTH — A SHARED SNAPSHOT FILE WOULD NEED A READ-MODIFY-WRITE FROM TWO PROCESSES, AND A LOST UPDATE THERE WOULD REPORT A RUNNING SERVER AS STOPPED, WHICH IS THE EXACT STATE THE INTERLOCK EXISTS TO REFUSE. A SECOND LIVE HOLDER OF ONE ROLE IS NOW REFUSED BY NAME AND PID — THE "TWO DASHBOARDS OVER ONE AUTHORITY STORE" COLLISION `config/runtime-isolation.json` HAS NAMED SINCE D88 AND NOTHING ENFORCED. **LIVENESS IS OBSERVED, NEVER ASSUMED, AND THE OBSERVATION WRITES NOTHING**: A CRASHED PROCESS LEAVES ITS LOCK HELD, AND TREATING THAT AS "STILL RUNNING" WOULD MAKE RESTORE PERMANENTLY IMPOSSIBLE AFTER A CRASH — THE ONE MOMENT IT IS MOST NEEDED — SO THE OS IS ASKED WHETHER THE PID EXISTS AND `stopped` IS REPORTED ONLY FOR A PID GENUINELY GONE (`EPERM` COUNTS AS **ALIVE**), WITH ANOTHER PROCESS'S LOCK FILE NEVER REWRITTEN. `runtimeGeneration` IS THE SUM OF THE TWO LOCK GENERATIONS, SO IT ADVANCES ON EVERY RESTART AND FENCES A PROOF ISSUED BEFORE ONE. **THE COMMAND NEVER CHOOSES A BACKUP**: `--from` NAMES ONE EXACT MANIFEST, BECAUSE "RESTORE THE NEWEST" IS A DECISION ABOUT WHICH MINUTES OF REAL HR WORK TO DISCARD; PROJECTION REBUILD RUNS AGAINST THE **STAGED** COPY THROUGH THE DASHBOARD'S OWN `createRunQueueProjection`, AND A DEGRADED REBUILD REFUSES THE WHOLE RESTORE WHILE THE LIVE AUTHORITY IS STILL UNTOUCHED, BECAUSE `rebuildFromJsonl` RECORDS RATHER THAN THROWS AND WITHOUT THAT CHECK THE OPERATOR WOULD BE HANDED A DATABASE WHOSE SURFACES ARE SILENTLY EMPTY. A REFUSAL IS **REPORTED** WITH ITS WHOLE `cause` CHAIN, THE OBSERVED LOCK LINE AND EXIT 1 — NOT A STACK TRACE — BECAUSE A RUNNING RUNTIME IS AN EXPECTED OUTCOME THE OPERATOR MUST READ. THE DRILL RAN ON A **COPY OF THE STANDING LOCAL AUTHORITY DATABASE** (NEVER IN PLACE) AND ITS MEASURED NUMBERS ARE IN THE OWNING DOC; IT FOUND ONE DEFECT — A `StorageHealthCause.summary` IS A ONE-LINE SENTENCE PRINTED IN THE DOCTOR REPORT, SETTINGS AND A NOTIFICATION TITLE, YET THE FAILURES PRODUCING IT ARE OFTEN ZOD ERRORS WHOSE `message` IS MULTI-LINE JSON, SO THE FIRST RUN BURIED THE SENTENCE UNDER TWENTY LINES OF ISSUE OBJECTS; IT IS NOW COLLAPSED TO ONE LINE WITH EVERY WORD KEPT. | ref dashboard-surfaces §2.5 D181 (primary); ref guards-and-tests §3.10; `src/infra/sqlite/runtime-process-locks.ts`; `src/infra/sqlite/authority-boot.ts`; `src/server/storage/compose.ts`; `src/server/dashboard/compose.ts`; `src/server/worker/compose.ts`; `src/core/runtime-composition.ts`; `tests/unit/storage-restore-drill.test.ts` |

EVERY ENTRY IS RE-DECIDED AGAINST THE **TREE**, NEVER THE PLAN, UNDER ONE RULE: **A WRONG `native` CLAIM IS WORSE THAN AN HONEST `open`** — SO A FAMILY WHOSE CORE LANDED BUT WHOSE OPERATOR-VISIBLE HALF DID NOT (THE LOG PANEL WITHOUT ITS SCREENSHOT GALLERY, SETTINGS WITHOUT AN EDITOR, CAPTURE WITHOUT AN OPERATOR PHOTO REVIEW) STAYS OPEN AND **NAMES WHAT IS MISSING**. 27 CLOSE `native`/`replaced` WITH RESOLVING TARGETS AND ACCEPTED DIFFERENCES; ONE CLOSES `retired` (LEGACY'S DEV-ONLY `?view=` SCAFFOLDING — ITS `rebuild-demo` HALF WAS THE **PORT SOURCE** FOR THIS REPO'S SURFACES, NAMED IN THEIR PROVENANCE HEADERS, SO IT CANNOT EXIST HERE); 50 STAY OPEN AND MOVE FORWARD **WITH THE REASON RECORDED**. THREE VOCABULARY ADDITIONS, ALL DOC-07-SANCTIONED: `open` GAINS A REQUIRED `reason` (A BARE `{kind,milestone}` IS *HOW* THE DRIFT HAPPENED — NOTHING CONTRADICTED IT), `retired` JOINS THE USED DISPOSITIONS, AND `status` BECOMES AN EXACT PROJECTION OF THE DISPOSITION RATHER THAN A TWO-VALUED FLAG THAT WOULD HAVE TO CALL A RETIREMENT "IMPLEMENTED". THE GUARD NOW PARSES THE FILE WITH A RECURSIVELY STRICT ZOD UNION — **WHICH IS THE PROXY-FREE ARM**, SINCE `proxy` IS NOT IN IT — AND RUNS FIVE AUDITS: STATUS/KIND AGREEMENT BOTH WAYS, `ownerMilestone` EQUALS THE DECIDING DISPOSITION'S MILESTONE, EVERY `native`/`replaced` TARGET RESOLVES TO A GIT-VISIBLE PATH IN **THIS** REPO, NO ENTRY IS `open` AT OR BEFORE THE RUNTIME'S `completedThrough`, AND EVERY EVIDENCE TOKEN PARSES AS ONE OF FOUR CHECKABLE FORMS — WITH A DECIDED ENTRY REQUIRED TO CITE **REBUILD** PROOF, BECAUSE "THE LEGACY FILE STILL EXISTS" WAS THE ONLY EVIDENCE 73 ENTRIES CARRIED AND IT PROVES NOTHING ABOUT THIS REPO. **THE COMPLETED SET IS DERIVED, NOT TYPED:** DOC 07'S ORDERED WORK ITEMS ARE THE GUARD'S ONE MILESTONE ENUM AND THE MARKER COMES FROM INVOKING THE REAL `phase` COMMAND, SO **CLOSING A TAIL IS WHAT ARMS THE NEXT SWEEP** — ADVANCING `COMPLETED_THROUGH` IS THE ACT THAT FORCES THE NEXT RE-DISPOSITION, FAILING LOUD BY ENTRY ID. ZERO ENTRIES ARE NOW OPEN AT OR BEFORE THE MARKER. **THE FILE GATES CUTOVER, NOT A TAIL** (DOC doc 05 §Exit criteria'S WHOLE-PROGRAM FLIP), AND §2'S ROW 3 ALREADY NAMES THE HOME FOR EVERYTHING PHASE 2 DOES NOT BUILD — PER-WORKFLOW MIGRATION **PLUS EXPLICIT NON-WORKFLOW CAPABILITY CLOSURE (§3.6)**, WHOSE EXIT REQUIRES EVERY ENTRY TO REACH native/replaced/retired. ASSIGNING 49 LEGACY OPERATOR SURFACES TO "MILESTONE 2i" WAS AN EARLY 1a GUESS MADE LONG BEFORE 2i WAS SLICED, AND THE OPERATOR'S ACTUAL 2i (2i-A…2i-G, D168–D180) NEVER CONTAINED THEM — SO ALL SEVENTEEN STRANDED ENTRIES ARE RE-MILESTONED TO `phase-3-workflow-migration`, **A RE-MILESTONING AND NEVER A RE-CLASSIFICATION**: EVERY ONE KEEPS `status:"open"`, AND NOTHING WAS PROMOTED TO `native`/`replaced` TO MAKE A GATE GREEN. ## Reconciliation round 47 (2026-08-12) — operator decisions: the scope, the storage stance, and the Phase-3 entry

_phase: 3b_

| # | Decision | Owner (normative text) |
|---|---|---|
| **D193** | **ONE OPERATOR SITTING, THREE CLAUSES, ONE NUMBER — THE SCOPE IS SOLO/LOCAL/NEVER-HOSTED, STORAGE IS DONE UNTIL ASKED, AND PHASE 3 ENTERS AT SEPARATIONS.** (a) **SCOPE REAFFIRMED**: the system is and stays fully local to the one person using it, solo-operator only, and is NEVER hosted; "production" in these plans means the D89 day this repo becomes the operator's daily LOCAL tool, never a hosting platform. Two items are named as LATER with no owner and no design — packaging it as a locally-run app ("web app → real app"), and a cooperation/multi-user mode built ON TOP OF the solo mode if ever; the four D75 seams stay because they are already built and keep that possible, but NOTHING is designed for multi-user now. (b) **STORAGE STANCE — BECAUSE THIS DATABASE STORES RECORDS OF *COMPLETIONS*, NOT PRIMARY RECORDS.** The real record of a completed HR transaction lives in the EXTERNAL system (UCPath, Kuali, …) and can be checked there in more than one way; these rows carry HOW a thing was completed and WHETHER it completed, and once it is completed and approved they mean little going forward — which is what makes backup non-critical at this scope. A manual folder copy of `.tracker-rebuild/` with the app stopped is therefore an acceptable backup, and the 14/8/5 retention-sweep SCHEDULING and the content-store refcount reclamation move from "deferred with an owner" to **`wont-build-unless-asked`** — off every milestone, blocking no gate. It says nothing about durability DURING a write: the fence, its binding proof, and a parked intent stay load-bearing for the run in flight (ref write-safety). Everything built (dirty-only 15-min verified backup cadence, doctor, `cli storage restore`, the sweep function and its refusals) STAYS; the decision is about further investment, not about correctness. (c) **PHASE-3 ENTRY = SEPARATIONS (order 8)**, the choice §3.1 always left to the operator: a big multi-system workflow FORCES the store task libraries into existence (UCPath termination write family, Kuali read + finalization write family, and the new-kronos driver/session/timecard reads that do not exist at all today), so one migration mints what every later one reuses instead of trickling stores in through small reads. §3.3 is NOT re-sequenced — it stays the risk-proof ordering — and separations owes every §3.2 criterion, questionnaire first; its controlled-commit target is operator work at step 5 and is deliberately NOT invented. | (a) ref charter §Non-negotiables (primary); ref operator-trust §7; ref frontend-backend-integration §6.9 (the byte path is the LOCAL app's own bundle, ngrok stays the one internet-exposed path for phone capture only) · (b) ref operator-trust §2.2 (primary); ref dashboard-surfaces §2.5 D181 remainders · (c) doc 03c §Phase summary entry block (primary); doc 03c §Unverified candidate evidence - not a phase order note; `docs/rebuild/migrations/separations.md` (the first Phase-3 migration doc + its §b questionnaire) |

## Reconciliation round 48 (2026-08-12) — operator decision: the rich-extraction store convention

_phase: 3b (D194/D196) · 3a (D195)_

| # | Decision | Owner (normative text) |
|---|---|---|
| **D194** | **A READ TASK'S IDENTITY IS A PAGE VISIT, NOT A CALLER'S FIELD LIST — THE RICH-EXTRACTION CONVENTION IS RATIFIED.** One task per page visit, returning the **full useful surface** that page carries into **one FIXED strict** output schema; consumers **project** downstream (a field-path bind or a pure mini-store fold) and never re-scrape. The literal `read(fields…)` shape is **banned**: a contract has ONE output schema and ONE schema hash, and runs, checkpoints, terminal results and evidence receipts all bind that hash plus the produced digest (D110/D114/D82), so an output shape that is a function of a runtime argument is either a wide all-optional record that no longer proves what was read (and would fail `strict-boundary-schemas`) or a contract per field set. Two costs are accepted at authoring time, both one-time and both already proven shapes in this tree: every field that can legitimately be blank declares an explicit `{state:"absent", reason}` arm — because provenance is validated to cover EVERY canonical output leaf on every run, so a rich field with no absent arm can fail a workflow that never needed it — and every leaf declares its redaction classification up front (D140). Two genuinely different page visits stay two tasks; the convention groups by VISIT and never merges a second navigation into the first. **Scope: all NEW tasks from 2026-08-12 and every task a migration authors from order 8 onward. Existing tasks are NOT rewritten** — a store merges its duplicates when it is next touched for other reasons, because re-authoring a shipped output schema moves a hash existing run records already bind. The measured motivator: `crm/read-onboarding-record` and `crm/read-onboarding-dates` share an entire page visit and the record page already holds `First Day of Service`, which the record task drops and the dates task re-runs the whole search to read again. | ref task-contract §2.8 (primary) · ref stores §1 "How stores work" (derived view) · `docs/rebuild/migrations/separations.md` §2.6 (the options, the measured evidence, and the operator's ratification) |
| **D195** | **THE PANEL REGION IS A GRID, THE TWO SIDE PANELS SHARE ONE 376px TOKEN, AND NOTHING-SELECTED IS A STATE.** The queue/detail region was authored as two `flex-1` children; `flex-1` distributes free space only among children that EXIST, so with no run open the detail child was not rendered, nothing claimed the space, and `QueuePanel`'s root — a flex item with no width — shrank to its content, leaving a ~470px column of rows and **~950px of empty black at 1440×900 at every viewport since 1i-spine**. It is now `grid-cols-[var(--ds-w-side-panel)_minmax(0,1fr)]` above 1180 and one column below: a grid's tracks exist whether or not anything is placed in them, which is what makes the failure unreachable rather than merely fixed. Two consequences ride with it. (a) **The 400/470 queue pair of ref dashboard-surfaces §9.9 is SUPERSEDED** by one shared `--ds-w-side-panel` (376px) for the queue and the context rail, so neither side panel grows independently and the centre stays the largest column — the visual oracle (ref frontend-backend-integration §1) moved first and the table had not followed; the superseded measurements are retained in-place as the record of what was tried. (b) **`No run open` is an empty STATE** owing ref dashboard-surfaces §9's three things, not an absent element. The floating Workflow Panel also moves INSIDE the region, which is what stops it clipping the status-filter bar above, and it renders on the queue view only — a takeover replaces the queue whole, so a workflow switcher hovering over it would offer to rescope a surface that no longer has a scope. **The corpus recorded this defect rather than catching it**: pixels at `maxDiffPixels: 0` and the a11y half both passed over the empty column, the same shape as D187's two-pixel `LogPanel`, so the parity lane owes a geometry assertion before any reference is re-frozen. | ref dashboard-surfaces §9.9 (primary) · ref frontend-backend-integration §1 (the demo as visual oracle) · ref frontend-backend-integration §7 (the geometry assertion the lane owes) |
| **D196** | **THE FIRST STORE AUTHORED UNDER D194 SETTLES FIVE RULES FOR EVERY STORE SLICE AFTER IT — `new-kronos` IS SEMANTIC-READY AND STRUCTURALLY READ-ONLY.** (1) **A NAVIGATION-PLUS-STATE-CHANGE SEQUENCE ON ONE PAGE IS ONE TASK**: Go To, the subject proof, the range apply and the grid read are one visit to the Timecard, so separations §2.4's five-task sketch (which predates D194, ratified later in the same document) becomes TWO tasks — the search-results panel and the timecard. (2) **A RICH READ OWES AN INVARIANT OVER ITS COMPLETENESS, NOT ONLY A WIDER SCHEMA**: WFD's grid is VIRTUALIZED (a 73-day window renders 51 date rows at `scrollTop: 0`), so legacy's single DOM read silently truncates and can regress the last day worked onto a plausible, wrong date; the task accumulates across scroll and then asserts that the day set covers the applied window exactly, refusing otherwise. (3) **COLUMNS BIND BY HEADER NAME**, never by legacy's fixed cell index, and a missing or ambiguous header is a loud refusal naming the observed header row. (4) **THE PAGE'S OWN SUBJECT IS AN OUTPUT**, not an internal check — the timecard read returns the EID and display name the page showed beside the expected ones (S37), while the `subject` binding still fails the run. (5) **A VALUE THE CLASSIFIER CANNOT CLASSIFY IS REPORTED, NEVER DROPPED** (`unclassifiedPayCodes`; the live window carried `Juneteenth Observed` and `Independence Day Observed`, neither matching the ported `/holiday/i` rule). Where a migration's answer is "all of these fail" but the values must stay distinguishable (§4b), the distinct DURABLE value is `FailureRecord.code` — `employee-not-found`, `search-returned-no-match`, `search-no-signal` — and a timeout is never a negative. **SESSION**: New Kronos does NOT hop through `a5.ucsd.edu`; its chain lands on ForgeRock under `*.prd.mykronos.com` serving the same UCSD credential form, and D120's two facts are the application-issued `kronosAuthToken` plus the absence of both the UCSD SSO form and ForgeRock's XUI shell — the IDP's own JWT was REJECTED as the positive fact because it proves the identity provider authenticated somebody and is scoped to a domain that includes the sign-on host. **PORT**: sixteen legacy selector keys move candidate→ported against a live 2026-08-12 read-only re-verification, four narrowing deliberately; legacy's entire moment-picker calendar path does NOT port, because `fill()` is reverted by the Angular digest while REAL KEYSTROKES stick — D150's live re-verification deleted a subsystem rather than merely catching drift. | ref task-contract §2.9 (primary) · ref execution-and-sessions §4.1 (the session facts) · `docs/rebuild/migrations/separations.md` §9 (the live map) and its chunk-1 as-built · `src/stores/new-kronos/CLAUDE.md` |

## Reconciliation round 49 (2026-08-12) — separations chunk 2: the Kuali separation-document read

_phase: 3b_

| **D197** | **THE FIRST STORE *CUSTOMIZED* UNDER D194 SETTLES FIVE MORE RULES — `kuali` GAINS A READ FAMILY BESIDE ITS WRITE FAMILY, AND §4a's MAP IS NOW EXHAUSTIVE FROM A LIVE CAPTURE.** (1) **A SIBLING TASK FAMILY EXTENDS THE ONE DRIVER DECLARATION, NEVER MINTS A SECOND** — `defineTask(driver, …)` binds the store's exact invariant declaration, so the separation read is a fifth task on the same `KualiRrssDocumentDriver`; its (much wider) SCHEMAS may live in their own module, its declaration may not. (2) **WHEN A READ AND A WRITE SHARE ONE SCREEN, CATALOG ATTRIBUTION SPLITS BY FACT, NOT BY SCREEN** — three facts are genuinely shared, fourteen are the read's alone, and `Save` stays attributed to the commit task alone because the catalog answers "what can click this?". (3) **A REGISTRY INVARIANT MEETS A SECOND LEGITIMATE CASE BY BECOMING PER-KIND, NOT BY BEING RELAXED** — "exactly one subject-authoritative observation" becomes one PER SUBJECT IDENTITY, because this read's subject is the DOCUMENT (`artifact`/`document-id`: the run names a document and DISCOVERS the person) while the write's stays the PERSON. (4) **A CONTROL LEGACY HAS NO KEY FOR STAYS DRIVER-INTERNAL** even when most of its neighbours port cleanly — a fabricated port row is worse than an unpublished element (D196's rule, applied in the harder direction). (5) **A PERSON FIELD'S ARM DISCRIMINATOR IS NOT THE PERSON** — declaring a `{state,value}` union wholesale `direct-identifier` ran `last4` over the discriminator and produced a live receipt reading `employee.name.state = "sent"`; only `value` is the identifier. Its general consequence: **an evidence-classification change moves receipt BYTES for every already-settled run**, and D82's insert correctly refuses the divergent rebuild by name. **§4a IS SETTLED WITH DATA**: the complete `Type of Termination` option list was captured live read-only from three real documents at two workflow steps — **16 options plus Kuali's `- - -` sentinel, identical on all three** — and the map is exact-match-only with no default arm and no substring tier. Two findings ride with it: **`Death` is not an option on this form at all** (§4a's worked example cannot arise from this dropdown), and **`Never Started Employment` is genuinely unmapped and legacy misfiles it** — it is in legacy's involuntary deny-list but absent from its `REASON_CODE_MAP`, so legacy files `UC_INVOL_TERM` with the VOLUNTARY default `Resign - No Reason Given`. A third: **S13 was never an optional field** — legacy asks for the accessible name `"Location  *"` and the live name is `"Location*"`, so its recipe matched zero nodes on every document and its catch turned that into `location = ""`. | ref task-contract §2.10 (primary) · `docs/rebuild/migrations/separations.md` §9.3 (the live map + the captured option list) and §9.4 (the live proof) · ref stores §2 (`kuali`, `separations`) · `src/stores/kuali/CLAUDE.md` |
| **D198** | **A WIRE CONTRACT IS CONNECTED IN BOTH DIRECTIONS OR IT IS ON THE BOOKS — AND THE FIRST ENFORCEMENT WIRED ONE HALF AND DELETED ANOTHER.** The tree shipped the same defect twice with every gate green: a run-detail `captures` arm fully schema'd that `availableSections` could never return, and a bootstrap `"degraded"` arm the producer was typed to never emit. The `wire-connectivity` guard now closes the class: every `RunDetailSectionResponseSchema` arm must be returnable by `availableSections` (proven by CALLING it over records covering every lifecycle state, with the sweep asserting its own state coverage), every `ActionCommandKeySchema` literal must round-trip the kernel's command union (kernel-only commands carry a reasoned closed allowlist), and every client-referenced path must resolve to a served route — with `config/pending-wire-bindings.json` naming each ratified deferral by decision id, checked STALE in both directions (an entry whose arm became reachable, or vanished, fails). Its first run caught the four predicted section orphans (`review`, `people`, `context-evidence`, `captures` — all D184) plus one nobody predicted (`GET /api/v1/capture/sessions/:sessionId` served, never called). Three consequences landed with it. **(a)** The parked-write section is WIRED: `parked-write` is served exactly when the lifecycle says so, from the durable intent re-read per request (absence throws — a parked run with no intent is a lie, not an empty panel), with `resolve-write-present`/`resolve-write-absent` served as confirm-first descriptors — ref write-safety §4.1's operator resolutions are addressable by a surface for the first time. The negative-settlement `wait` block is NOT served because it is not durable anywhere (`createWriteRecovery` deliberately writes nothing on `wait`); serving it would have minted the exact unemittable arm this guard exists to catch, so it is a declared-unserved manifest entry instead. **(b)** The bootstrap `"degraded"` arm is DELETED, not wired: the only third state in the tree (`AuthorityBootStore`'s degraded-read-only) holds no database handle, which is what `unavailable` already says, and the storage doctor keeps the degraded cause; a schema-arm emittability test pins every remaining arm to a producer state. **(c)** As-built record for D195's detail region: the split keys on the COLUMN via container queries (thresholds derived `1280−412`/`1480−412` and pinned by test to the exported constants), `useContextRail` reads the same box the CSS measures instead of asking `matchMedia` about the window, and ONE viewport query survives on the container declaration (`min-[1180px]`) because below the shell's own stack breakpoint the region has no cell to measure. Found-not-fixed, on the books: `createWriteRecovery(...).recover` has NO production caller (ref write-safety §4's crash-window loop is itself unwired), and the corpus renders `contractFingerprint` as text so ANY descriptor-closure edit drifts 63 a11y references — a corpus property needing its own decision. | ref frontend-backend-integration §8 (guard, primary) + §3.1 (storage arm) + §3.4/§5 (parked-write section + resolve descriptors) · ref write-safety §4.1 (now-reachable resolutions; §4 recover-unwired finding) · ref dashboard-surfaces §9.9 (container-query as-built) · `config/pending-wire-bindings.json` (the deferral book) |

## Reconciliation round 50 (2026-08-12) — the two platform items chunk 2 surfaced

_phase: 3b_

| # | Decision | Owner (normative text) |
